## 2.13 HDE-CRD-0002 WU-PR-07: the closeout-validation job installs the repository's runtime requirements, so its read-only evidence checks can pass

Timestamp: 102026 15:50 (UTC). Details: Records the whole-change Implementation Agent's approval, through Review Bounded Work-Unit Rescope, of one bounded implementation rescope of work unit WU-PR-07 (requirement S21: the close-pack writer for Epic and CRD identities, the close-run evidence home and the ADR draft) of the approved HDE-CRD-0002 Implementation Plan v1.1.

* Origin: the WU-PR-07 rescope proposal v1.0 (finding F-1, items D-1 and D-2) from the dedicated PR engineering session during pre-Proceed planning, written at the Product Owner's direction "Option A, write the rescope proposal".  
* Evidence: the review's own reproduction at main a9fd343, in throwaway clones with two fresh interpreters, of the failing step and its correction; the plan v1.0 rehearsal of the close sequence; the GitHub Actions record of the workflow (0 runs).

### Decision and approval status

The continuing whole-change Implementation Agent for HDE-CRD-0002 decided APPROVE on the WU-PR-07 rescope proposal v1.0, as one state for the one proposal. The decision record is docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-WU-PR-07-rescope-review-v1.0.md.  
The approval is a bounded implementation rescope within the approved Specification's intent. It does not change the Epic-level commitment, any Specification acceptance criterion, any protected boundary, any other work unit, any accepted dependency or any Product Owner matter. The immutable base is not rewritten.  
This addendum is the one overlay the approval creates. The effective baseline for WU-PR-07 is the approved base, plus addenda 2.11 (read with 2.12's numbering), 2.12, 2.5 and 2.3, plus the Product Owner's business-case disposition of 2026-10-20, plus this addendum.  
No canon-conflict register entry is created or changed.

### Base

The base is not changed. Its relevant clauses are:

* Specification v1.1: requirement S21 and its acceptance intent ("The writer produces a canon-conforming close report and manifest from tracked inputs for an Epic or a CRD; close-workflow run evidence has a home …; the accompanying ADR is approved"); the constraint "Close-pack writer", which states that "S21 touches the release-sanity and close-validation lanes".  
* Implementation Plan v1.1, WU-PR-07:  
  * Deliverables, which name .github/workflows/epic-closeout-validation.yml "only where they read the schema version";  
  * Terminal-state reachability (e): HDE-CRD-0002's close dispatches that workflow on the exact close head (T7) before the Product Owner merges.  
* WU-PR-07 PR instruction v1.0:  
  * §6 lists the workflow as expected unchanged;  
  * §7: "No CI change. No new workflow, job, step or lane; the validation workflow and release-sanity stages stay as they are (ID-10)";  
  * ID-10: "The job also runs the updater's \--check, orientation\_demo.py \--check, the hash, path, Mirror-schema and final-LF checks. ID-9 keeps them passing before HDE-CRD-0002's close pack exists";  
  * contract H-6;  
  * §14 names a needed change to the validation workflow as an expected rescope trigger.

### Finding

The closeout-validation job cannot pass its step "Run retained read-only evidence checks" for any candidate.

* The install. The job installs only jsonschema==4.23.0.  
* The import. tools/evidence/update\_evidence\_index.py \--check runs \_validate\_epic038\_pr02\_semantics, which imports tools/evidence/generate\_a7\_transport\_proofs.py. That reaches adapter/http\_reader.py, which imports Flask at module level.  
* Measured at main a9fd343, under the job's own environment with a jsonschema-only interpreter: the updater's \--check exits 1 with ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'flask'. The other five read-only checks exit 0\.  
* On the plan's rehearsal of a real close state, the writer's \--check exits 0 and the updater's \--check fails the same way.  
* History. The import entered the updater on 2026-07-28 (\#375), before the workflow was added on 2026-08-21 (\#396, HDE-EPIC039 PR-05). The step has therefore never been passable. The workflow has never been dispatched.  
* Effect. Without a change, T7 fails for HDE-CRD-0002's close and for every later close. The business-case measure for WU-PR-07 ("every check mode passes … on the first attempt") cannot be met.  
* Instruction premise. Instruction v1.0 ID-10's statement that those checks pass was not measured under the job's install and is false.

### Approved delta

1. D-1. In .github/workflows/epic-closeout-validation.yml, the install step changes from \- name: Install bounded validation dependency / run: python \-m pip install 'jsonschema==4.23.0' to \- name: Install the read-only checks' runtime requirements / run: python \-m pip install \-r requirements.txt. Nothing else in the workflow changes: the triggers, inputs, permissions (contents: read), checkout (persist-credentials: false, exact ref), environment, step count and order, the writer's \--check, the six read-only checks and the clean-checkout assertions all stay as they are.  
2. D-2. In tests/evidence/test\_rails\_ci\_workflow\_integration.py, the test test\_manual\_closeout\_validation\_is\_isolated\_read\_only\_and\_exact\_head gains four lines. They assert that the workflow has exactly one python \-m pip install, and that it is run: python \-m pip install \-r requirements.txt.  
3. Bounded files. WU-PR-07's bounded files gain exactly those two paths, each for exactly that change.  
4. Overlaid instruction clauses. Instruction v1.0 is read as follows for WU-PR-07:  
   * §6: the validation workflow leaves the expected-unchanged list for D-1 only. .github/workflows/ci.yml, every other workflow and tools/evidence/run\_sanity\_pipeline.py stay expected unchanged.  
   * §7: "No new workflow, job, step or lane; the release-sanity stages stay as they are; the validation workflow changes only by D-1 (its install step installs requirements.txt)."  
   * ID-10: the job installs requirements.txt, and its six read-only checks pass at the base and at the prototype head. ID-9 keeps them passing before HDE-CRD-0002's close pack exists. The writer and the v2 schema still keep standard-library plus jsonschema imports.  
   * Contract H-6: "unchanged inputs; the job installs requirements.txt (D-1); the writer stays runnable with jsonschema==4.23.0 alone".  
   * §14: the rescope trigger for a needed change to the validation workflow no longer covers D-1. Any other change to that workflow remains a trigger.  
5. Validation. The unit's validation adds three checks:  
   * the job's seven checks, run with a fresh interpreter holding only \-r requirements.txt on the rehearsal's terminal state, each exiting 0 with the checkout clean afterwards;  
   * the writer's \--check, run on that state with a jsonschema-only interpreter, exiting 0;  
   * D-2's negative control: the new assertions fail against the unchanged workflow, and the test file passes with D-1 and D-2.  
6. Plan v1.1. The unit's next planning artifact is plan v1.1, a complete successor of plan v1.0. It carries D-1 and D-2, F-1's disposition and the updated ceiling statement. Plan v1.0 and its prototype diff are preserved.

### Evidence

Each check below was run by the reviewer in throwaway clones under the job's environment: LC\_ALL=C LANG=C TZ=UTC SAFE\_MODE=1 ALLOW\_NETWORK=0 APP\_ENV=dev PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 GIT\_OPTIONAL\_LOCKS=0 GIT\_NO\_LAZY\_FETCH=1, with CPython 3.12.3.

| Check | Result |
| :---- | :---- |
| The six read-only checks at a9fd343, jsonschema-only interpreter | the updater's \--check exits 1 (No module named 'flask'); the other five exit 0; checkout clean |

## 2.14 A P1 finding is addressed until it is resolved; only a finding that depends on an input the product will never have may be declined

Timestamp: 102026 18:55 (UTC). Details: Records the Product Owner's rule that no P1 review finding is declined, deferred, or accepted as a stated limit while its triggering input is real; the one exception is a finding whose triggering input the product will never have. Origin: the Product Owner's direction of 2026-10-20 in the continuing HDE-CRD-0002 Implementation Agent session, after the WU-PR-07 PR-35 session offered to decline two P1 Codex findings (CX7-2 and CX7-3 on pull request [amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2\#668](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/668)) as stated limits. Evidence: the PR-35 session's decision request and the two findings, both checked against the code on the pull request's head and against Change Process Guide §3.5.2.1, which requires the behaviour each finding reports missing.

### Source and authority

This addendum records the Product Owner's rule, in the Product Owner's words: "I do NOT want to ignore P1 findings ever. If we have some rule to do that now, it is wrong" and "P1s need to be addressed until the end, unless the violate the rule where they are testing for inputs that will never exist in this app." The Product Owner directed that this be recorded as an HDE Build Notes addendum. Where this addendum conflicts with earlier PF-Canon, an earlier addendum or an in-flight change document, this addendum governs for the scope below.

### Rule

1. Scope. A P1 finding is any review finding labelled P1, or any higher priority such as P0, by the Codex code review or by a person, on any pull request of any Epic or CRD.  
2. Addressed until resolved. A P1 finding whose triggering input is real is fixed. It is not declined, deferred to a later change, recorded as a stated limit, or resolved on cost, effort, design preference, a plan's earlier choice, an ADR's stated limit, or a "not worth its code" judgement.  
   * Fixed means the wrong result, crash or guarantee breach the finding reports no longer occurs on that real input. A test shows it, and the test fails without the fix.  
   * A typed refusal of the input is a fix when refusing it is correct behaviour for the product.  
   * The thread stays open until the fix is pushed and the finding's own check of it is answered. A pull request with an open P1 thread on a real input is not merge-ready.  
3. The one exception. A P1 finding may be declined only when its triggering input is not real under addendum 2.11 rule 4: it does not exist on main or in an approved unit's planned deliverable, does not occur in the product's real operating environment, and cannot be supplied by a user, vendor or attacker through an interface the product exposes. The input is then one constructed only to exercise the code (addendum 2.11 rule 5).  
   * The reply on the thread gives the traced path and shows why the input can never occur in the product.  
   * As before, declining a P1 goes to the Product Owner first, and the thread is resolved only after that.  
4. Repeated finding class. Addendum 2.11 rule 8 still applies: two consecutive rounds raising findings of the same class stop the fixing of instances, and the session asks the Product Owner one design question. For a P1 on a real input, "accept a stated limit" is not an option. The answer is a fix or a narrowed design that removes the wrong result, including a typed refusal, or a rescope through the native route. In every case the P1 on its real input is resolved before the pull request merges.  
5. Budget. A P1 fix that would pass a unit's cost ceiling (addendum 2.11 rules 1 and 3\) still returns to the Product Owner for budget direction before work continues. That return is about the budget. It does not make declining the P1 an option.  
6. Unchanged.  
   * Findings below P1 keep addendum 2.11 rule 7 as written.  
   * Security findings below P1 keep rule 7's Product Owner-first rule for a decline.  
   * The real-input reporting rule for reviewers (AGENTS.md "Code review guidelines") is unchanged.  
   * Nothing here weakens the existing duty to fix a real defect at any priority.

### Scope and supersession

* Applies from this addendum to every open and future pull request of every Epic and CRD, including in-flight HDE-CRD-0002 units. That includes pull request [\#668](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/668) (WU-PR-07), whose P1 findings CX7-2 and CX7-3 rest on real inputs: a close candidate source whose tracked issues carry an ADR status, and one that omits an acceptance row for a requirement in the bound Specification. Both are therefore fixed.  
* Supersedes addendum 2.11 rule 7 only where it allows declining a P1 on a real input: its sentence "Otherwise the reply on the thread gives the traced path and the stated limit, and the thread is resolved", as applied to a P1, and its sentence "Declining a P1 or a security finding goes to the Product Owner first", as it would let a P1 on a real input be declined.  
* Supersedes addendum 2.11 rule 8's option "accept a stated limit" for a P1 on a real input.  
* Supersedes any plan, instruction, ADR stated limit or Product Owner disposition recorded before this addendum, to the extent it would leave a P1 on a real input unfixed.  
* Does not change addendum 2.11 rules 1 to 6, the real-input definition, or rule 7 for findings below P1.  
* Does not reopen accepted-final work. Earlier declines remain historical facts, for example WU-PR-06's three declined findings, which were P2.

### Epic, task, and status effects

* No epic, CRD, task, PF09 or PF30 row changes status by this addendum.  
* WU-PR-07 (pull request [\#668](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/668)): CX7-2 and CX7-3 are fixed in the PR-35 session, as the Product Owner directed on 2026-10-20. The ADR's stated limit "Requirement coverage is the source author's" is removed.

### Required canon drainage

Drainage is unperformed.

| Target | Required change |
| :---- | :---- |
| PR-35 — Resolve PR Reviews and Reach Merge Readiness — 100926.1, step 3 | State that a P1 on a real input is fixed and never declined, and that only a P1 on a non-real input may be declined, with the Product Owner first (owner GCFPE-MGMT-10 and the Product Owner) |
| PR-40 — Review PR Work-Unit Lineage — 100926.1 | Check that no P1 on a real input was declined before merge |
| Plan Templates "Source-bound review authority and exact-fix limits" and Change Process Guide §1.1.4 | Carry rules 2 and 3 beside addendum 2.11's rules 7 and 8 |
| AGENTS.md "Code review guidelines" | Optional: note that a P1 the reviewer reports on a real input is fixed by the PR session. Editing AGENTS.md needs the Product Owner's explicit instruction |

### Deferred obligations and unresolved work

* The prompt and canon drainage above: the Product Owner and GCFPE-MGMT-10.  
* Publication of this addendum into PF10, after addendum 2.13: the Product Owner.

### Scope boundaries and nonclaims

* This addendum changes no prompt, skill, AGENTS.md or permanent PF document; the drainage above is unperformed.  
* It establishes no QA, acceptance, merge or closure, and it does not decide any finding's priority. The priority is the label the reviewer gives.

### Evidence anchors

* The Product Owner's words, quoted under "Source and authority".  
* PF10 addendum 2.11 rules 4, 5, 7 and 8 (on main, v13.6.3).  
* Pull request [\#668](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/668): Codex findings CX7-2 (tracked-issue ADR statuses not checked against the drain-targets ledger) and CX7-3 (SATISFIED without an acceptance row for every requirement). On its head a6c6655, tools/qa/generate\_epic\_close\_pack.py checks only deferrals against the drain-targets ledger. Change Process Guide §3.5.2.1 requires a TI item's ADR status line to record "Deferred with drain targets", and the acceptance section to bind "each current governing requirement or acceptance criterion".

### Relationship to existing HDE Build Notes guidance

The addendum narrows addendum 2.11 rules 7 and 8 for P1 findings, as stated under "Scope and supersession", and is otherwise consistent with addenda 2.3, 2.11, 2.12 and 2.13. It keeps 2.11's real-input definition as the only ground for declining a P1.

## 2.15 HDE-CRD-0002 WU-PR-07: the Product Owner approves the S21 close-pack writer ADR with changes, and the close-run evidence family is introduced

Timestamp: 102026 23:40 (UTC). Details: Records the Product Owner's AQ-1 decision on the S21 architecture decision record, docs/adr/hde/close\_pack\_writer\_adr.md, of work unit WU-PR-07 of the approved HDE-CRD-0002 Implementation Plan v1.1. The record is approved with changes. This addendum also introduces the post-verdict close-run evidence family that HDE Schemas & Artifacts §8.17 requires a Build Notes addendum to introduce. Origin: the AQ-1 proposal v1.0 from the WU-PR-07 PR-35 session, the Implementation Agent's review of it, and the Product Owner's choice in the Implementation Agent session on 2026-10-20. Evidence: the review's checks of the ADR, the v2 writer and canon at pull request [amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2\#668](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/668)'s head 45e4cef.

### Decision and approval status

* Decision. AQ-1 is decided: the S21 ADR is approved with changes. The decision is the Product Owner's (Implementation Plan v1.1 §13; HDE Governance §9.7.3). It satisfies Change Process Guide §1.0.5's requirement of an approved ADR for S21's canon change, on condition that changes C-1 to C-4 below land in pull request [\#668](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/668) before it merges.  
* What is approved. The design of the ADR at [\#668](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/668) head 45e4cef:  
  * one writer at tools/qa/generate\_epic\_close\_pack.py with a second, closed source schema schemas/close\_candidate\_source.v2.json for Epic (HDE-EPICNNN) and CRD (HDE-CRD-NNNN) identities, v1 unchanged;  
  * CRD paths of record audit/HDE-CRD-NNNN\_close\_report.md and audit/HDE-CRD-NNNN\_MANIFEST.json, with the directory form hde-crd-nnnn;  
  * the v2 report and manifest rendered only from tracked inputs, with a key\_outputs map of path strings;  
  * the feedback-dependency refusal kept, and requirement text bound by reference;  
  * the post-verdict close-run family (below);  
  * absent-tolerant CRD close-pack Index rows;  
  * the dependency direction: causal inputs → close report and manifest → Index; close-run primaries → Index.  
* Not approved as drafted. The proposal's §6 text delta is replaced by redlines R-1 to R-4 of the review.  
* Status line. When C-1 to C-4 are pushed, the ADR's status line reads: Status: Approved with changes by the Product Owner on 2026-10-20 (AQ-1; HDE Build Notes addendum 2.15; review docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-WU-PR-07-adr-review-v1.0.md). The WU-PR-07 PR-35 session writes that line as a transcription of this decision. Instruction v1.0 ID-12's "no agent marks the ADR approved" is satisfied because the Product Owner, not the agent, made the decision and gave its text here.

### Required changes before [\#668](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/668) merges

1. C-1. ADR block. Change Process Guide §3.5.4 requires an ADR block in every close report, and HDE Schemas & Artifacts §1.2 "Change-container compatibility" applies it to a CRD close.  
   * The v2 schema gains a required, non-empty list of ADR-block entries. Each entry has a label, a statement, the decision point, the options considered when more than one was real, the bounding PF titles, the final decision, whether it becomes canonical or stays change-specific, and the doc-delta targets by PF title.  
   * An optional closure-override entry enumerates accepted gaps with their evidence basis and drain targets.  
   * The report renders the entries as an ADR block with titles-only canon references.  
2. C-2. The current approved Specification. The requirements document a v2 source binds must be its own change's docs/ephemeral/\<change-id\>-specification-v\<N\>.\<N\>-approved.md, at the highest version tracked. Any other document is refused with one typed code.  
   * Both real approved Specifications follow that pattern: HDE-CRD-0002 v1.1 and HDE-EPIC040 v1.1.  
   * A superseded Specification, such as HDE-CRD-0002 v1.0 with 21 requirement rows, can then no longer support a SATISFIED close.  
3. C-3. Tracked-issue fields. A tracked-issue item renders HDE Governance §2.0.19's literal canon-pointer fields: TI-\<id\> pointers: for a pointer item, and ADR status line: for an ADR-status item.  
4. C-4. ADR text. The ADR's Decision section and canon table carry redlines R-1 to R-4 of the review, together with C-1 to C-3. In summary:  
   * R-1: the binary decision and its posture; requirement IDs read from the current approved Specification in the change kind's pinned format; every criterion ID one of them; SATISFIED needs a met row for every ID and no follow-up, and NOT SATISFIED needs a follow-up;  
   * R-2: the drain-targets ledger names the deferral's or tracked issue's own ID;  
   * R-3: every key output other than the close report and manifest is also a bound tracked input;  
   * R-4: five further canon homes in the table.  
5. Validation.  
   * The negative controls, the writer test file, every updater consumer, and the evidence and qa lanes pass locally before one push.  
   * Hosted CI and a fresh Codex code review run on the resulting head.  
   * Every P1 finding on a real input is fixed (addendum 2.14).  
6. Scope. C-1 to C-3 are corrections within S21's acceptance intent ("a canon-conforming close report"), in files already in WU-PR-07's bounded set. They need no rescope. A projected pass of the unit's cost ceiling returns to the Product Owner before work continues (addendum 2.11 rule 3; addendum 2.14 rule 5).

### The close-run evidence family (introduced)

HDE Schemas & Artifacts §8.17 requires a new recurring QA evidence family to be introduced through a Build Notes addendum. This addendum introduces one:

* Path. audit/qa/\<change-dir\>/close/\<check\_id\>/primary.log, each with its sibling .path\_proof.txt, for a CRD admitted in the canonical updater's CRD admission table. \<change-dir\> is the lower-case directory form, for example hde-crd-0002.  
* What it holds. Same-run mechanical evidence of a change's close workflow: one primary per close-run check, rendered from that check's captured command, exit code and output as a PF27 v2 step-log header. Its posture is PASS, FAIL or BLOCKED, as Glow QA Guide "Close-pack truthfulness and same-run execution" requires.  
* Status. A post-verdict family. It is written after the final QA verdict, and it neither reopens nor adds to the change's approved qa\_step\_logs\_manifest.json or any checks/ primary.  
* Writer. Only the canonical updater, tools/evidence/update\_evidence\_index.py, writes it, through its close-run publication entrypoint inside one recoverable transaction. The given results replace the whole family. The entrypoint requires the change's recorded QA family to exist.  
* Membership. The family's members are the validated primaries and path proofs under close/, in a closed shape; anything else under close/ is refused.  
  * This is a scoped exception, for the close/ directory only, to §8.17 "Current-state evidence": "Tools MUST NOT infer run state by enumerating subdirectories". That rule continues to govern checks/ and every other part of a QA root, where qa\_step\_logs\_manifest.json stays the authoritative index.  
* Not admitted. No Epic close-run family. An Epic needs its own explicit admission later, as each CRD needs its admission row.

### Scope, exclusions and nonclaims

* This addendum produces no close pack, candidate source or close-run family for any change. HDE-EPIC040 gets no retroactive close pack.  
* It changes no Specification requirement or acceptance criterion, no other work unit, and no CI lane.  
* It establishes no QA verdict, acceptance, Ops, deployment, PF09 or PF30 movement, merge or closure.  
* Drainage of the ADR's affected canon homes is unperformed. Its absence is not a blocker of any change or close (HDE Governance §2.0.19; HDE Schemas & Artifacts §8.17).  
* Earlier canon governs except where this addendum speaks: the approval itself; the close-run family and its membership exception; the ADR status text.

### Dependencies, conflicts and unresolved obligations

* Dependencies.  
  * C-1 to C-4 land in [\#668](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/issues/668) in the WU-PR-07 PR-35 session.  
  * The Product Owner merges [\#668](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/issues/668) only after they land and the pull request is green.  
  * WU-PR-08 and the later units wait on that merge.  
* Conflicts. None. Where this addendum departs from HDE Schemas & Artifacts §8.17's no-enumeration rule, it does so only for the close/ family. The canon-conflict register (CR-1 to CR-8) is unchanged.  
* Unresolved, with owners.  
  * The C-1 to C-4 implementation and its records: the WU-PR-07 PR-35 session.  
  * Drainage of the ADR's canon table, including this family into HDE Schemas & Artifacts §8.17 and Glow QA Guide §3.4.3: the Product Owner, through the normal canon-maintenance route.  
  * Publication of this addendum into PF10, after 2.14: the Product Owner.

## 2.17 HDE-CRD-0002 WU-PR-07: the S21 close-pack writer ADR defers to HDE Build Notes; the Product Owner's amendments of 2026-10-21 are recorded and the ADR's contradictions are resolved

Timestamp: 102126 17:10 (UTC). Details: Records the Product Owner's rule that the S21 architecture decision record, docs/adr/hde/close\_pack\_writer\_adr.md, defers to HDE Build Notes. It also records his amendments of 2026-10-21 to the design that addendum 2.15 approved, and resolves the two contradictions that no addendum resolved.  
Origin: finding LR7-O1 of the WU-PR-07 PR-40 lineage review v1.0 (docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-WU-PR-07-pr-work-unit-lineage-review-v1.0.md), and the Product Owner's answer to it.  
Evidence: the ADR on main at 8b73b57; the landed writer, schema and tests; the PR-35 result records named below.

### Source and authority

This addendum records the Product Owner's rule, in his words (2026-10-21): "The adr document should defer to PF10. So if there is a contradiction unresolved by PF10, it needs a build notes addendum to resolve it."  
Where this addendum conflicts with the S21 ADR, with addendum 2.15 or with an in-flight change document, this addendum governs for the scope below.

### Rule

1. Precedence. The S21 ADR defers to HDE Build Notes. Where its text conflicts with an applicable addendum, the addendum governs.  
2. Unresolved contradictions. A contradiction that no addendum resolves is resolved by a new HDE Build Notes addendum. That covers a contradiction inside the ADR, between the ADR and an addendum, or between the ADR and a later Product Owner decision.  
3. The amended design. The design addendum 2.15 approved, at pull request \#668 head 45e4cef, is amended by the Product Owner's decisions in the table below.  
   * Each decision is the one recorded in the WU-PR-07 PR-35 result record named beside it.

The P1 fixes made under addendum 2.14 without a decision number also changed the ADR's text, and stand with the table:

* CX7-12 (result v1.11): the whole PF27 v2 header of each QA primary is checked;  
* CX7-14 (result v1.13): the later-drain epic-close expectation uses HDE Governance's three values;  
* CX7-19 (result v1.18): PF titles are read from every tracked PF-Canon file name.

### Resolutions

A. A TOOLING\_BLOCKED check under a closure override (PO-35-13).

* The contradiction. The ADR's stated limit on the QA step-logs manifest ends "and SATISFIED needs every recorded status to be PASS". That text came from PO-35-4. The ADR's decision 4 and its closure-override limit state PO-35-13 instead. PF10 did not decide between them.  
* Resolved. A v2 SATISFIED decision needs every check of the change's current QA plan to be recorded in the QA step-logs manifest in one of two ways:  
  * as PASS; or  
  * as TOOLING\_BLOCKED, where a closure-override gap in the ADR block names that check, once.  
* What blocks SATISFIED: a check recorded as FAIL\_BEHAVIOR, FAIL\_TOOLING or PARKED, and a planned check the manifest does not record.  
* Not PASS evidence: a TOOLING\_BLOCKED check accepted under an override is never PASS evidence for a MET criterion (PO-35-21).  
* Superseded: the stated-limit clause "and SATISFIED needs every recorded status to be PASS".  
* Basis: Change Process Guide §3.5.4, "Closure override" and "No posture-only PASS". The landed writer at 8b73b57 and its test test\_v2\_closure\_override\_exempts\_only\_the\_tooling\_blocked\_check\_it\_names already behave this way.

B. The tracked-issue pointer field of addendum 2.15 C-3 (PO-35-11).

* The contradiction. C-3 has a tracked-issue item render HDE Governance §2.0.19's TI-\<id\> pointers: field for a pointer item, and ADR status line: for an ADR-status item. PO-35-11 removed the pointer item from the v2 schema.  
* Resolved. For close\_candidate\_source.v2:  
  * a tracked-issue item is an ADR-status item only, and renders ADR status line:;  
  * C-3's pointer clause is superseded.  
* Consequence. A tracked issue closed as satisfied through PF09 or PF30 pointers cannot be expressed by the v2 writer: that is Change Process Guide §3.5.2.1 form (a), and HDE Governance §2.0.19's PF09 pointers: and TI-\<id\> pointers: fields. Such a close needs a later schema version.  
* Basis. The ADR records it: no PF09 or PF30 file cites a TI ID, so no pointer can show that issue's closure. Form (b), an ADR status line, satisfies §3.5.2.1.

### Scope and supersession

* Applies to the S21 ADR and to close\_candidate\_source.v2 and its writer, from this addendum on.  
* Supersedes:  
  * addendum 2.15 C-3, for pointer items only;  
  * the ADR clause named in resolution A.  
* Extends:  
  * addendum 2.15 C-1, by PO-35-13's naming rule;  
  * 2.15's "What is approved", by the table.  
* Does not change:  
  * the rest of addendum 2.15: the AQ-1 approval, the status line, C-1, C-2, C-4, the close-run evidence family and its membership exception;  
  * addenda 2.11, 2.13 and 2.14;  
  * any other ADR, schema or work unit.  
* Not reopened: WU-PR-07's delivery, which PR-40 lineage review v1.0 accepts.

### Epic, task, and status effects

* WU-PR-07: PR-40 lineage review v1.0 accepts the unit. Its finding LR7-O1 is resolved here, and its observation LR7-O9 is resolved as resolution B.  
* No code change is needed: the landed writer already implements both resolutions.

## 2.18 Requirements and work units carry no cost ceiling; work continues until it is done

Timestamp: 102126 19:07 (UTC). Details: Withdraws the cost ceiling that addendum 2.11 added to every requirement and work unit, together with every gate, return, recording duty and decision that depends on it. Removes nothing else. The business case keeps its beneficiary, evidence, benefit and measure, and the real-input, emulation, finding-disposition, repeated-finding-class and P1 rules stand. Origin: the Product Owner's direction of 2026-10-21 in the continuing HDE-CRD-0002 Lead Developer session (Isis, [https\://claude.ai/code/session\_012tPq26JWVhCYZmcqTidpV2](https://claude.ai/code/session_012tPq26JWVhCYZmcqTidpV2)). It followed the IA-30 change assessment 01 v1.1 question on WU-PR-08's ceiling.

### Source and authority

This addendum records the Product Owner's rule, in the Product Owner's words:

* "these ceiling concepts are practically meaningless. I don't know where they got introduced. Work is done until its done."  
* "ok create a new PF10 addendum. there is no value to me in this concept."

Where the ceiling came from: the HDE-CRD-0002 WU-PR-06 postmortem proposed "a cost ceiling per unit", and it entered HDE Build Notes as part of addendum 2.11. The Product Owner's own words recorded in 2.11 ("Every PR must have a business case. It cannot simply be for the sake of evidence or CI." and "no work is ever done based on inputs the product will never have.") do not mention a ceiling.  
Where this addendum conflicts with an earlier addendum or with an in-flight change document, this addendum governs for the scope below.

### Rule

1. No cost ceiling. A requirement or work unit states no cost ceiling: no budget in sessions, elapsed days, review rounds or pushes.  
   * The business case of addendum 2.11 rule 1 consists of its beneficiary, evidence, benefit and measure.  
   * The field "its cost ceiling: the most the work may spend before it returns to the Product Owner, in sessions and elapsed days" is withdrawn.  
2. Work continues until it is done. Spend, elapsed time and the number of sessions, review rounds or pushes are not, by themselves, a reason to stop work or return it to the Product Owner.  
   * Work returns to the Product Owner only where another rule sends it:  
     * addendum 2.11 rule 8 (repeated finding class);  
     * addendum 2.14 (a P1 decline);  
     * a material change, rescope or Product Owner decision required by the governing prompt or canon.  
   * A real need for Product Owner direction on scope or risk still goes to the Product Owner.  
3. Gates without a ceiling. In addendum 2.11 rule 3:  
   * Specification approval still blocks a requirement without a business case.  
   * IA-30 checks each work unit's business case.  
   * PR-10 carries the business case into the instruction.  
   * The Proceed is given against the business case.  
   * A unit whose business case is met still stops.  
   * Withdrawn:  
     * "and ceiling" in the IA-30 bullet;  
     * "both" in the PR-10 bullet, read as including the ceiling;  
     * "and PR-20 states how the plan stays within the ceiling";  
     * the whole bullet beginning "A unit that passes its ceiling".  
4. Internal tooling. Addendum 2.11 rule 2's last sentence reads "Internal tooling qualifies only when its expected saving, from observed incident data, exceeds its expected cost." Its comparison with a ceiling is withdrawn.  
5. No recording against a ceiling. No session, checkpoint, result record, lineage review or Product Owner decision records spend against a ceiling, projects a pass of one, or sets one. PR-40 still records whether the unit's measure was met.  
6. Unchanged. The following stand as written and as superseded before this addendum:  
   * addendum 2.11 rules 4 to 8;  
   * the real-input definition;  
   * addendum 2.14 rules 1 to 4 and 6;  
   * the CI-budget rules that addendum 2.11 left unchanged.

### Scope and supersession

Applies from this addendum to every Epic and CRD, including the remaining HDE-CRD-0002 units. Supersedes, for the cost-ceiling scope only:

* Addendum 2.11:  
  * rule 1's cost-ceiling bullet;  
  * rule 2's comparison "exceeds its ceiling" (restated by rule 4 above);  
  * rule 3's ceiling clauses and its passing-the-ceiling bullet;  
  * in "Deferred obligations and unresolved work", the clauses:  
    * "and ceiling" (IA-30);  
    * "and ceiling" (PR-10);  
    * "PR-30 and PR-35 record spend against the ceiling in each checkpoint";  
    * "the unit's actual cost against its ceiling and" (PR-40).  
* Addendum 2.12: the treatment of a missing ceiling as a gap (LR6-O3's "ceiling") and the "cost ceiling in sessions and elapsed days" in the decision returned for WU-PR-07.  
* Addendum 2.13: the ceiling statements and the rule that a projected pass of the ceiling is material. These are historical for the completed WU-PR-07.  
* Addendum 2.14: rule 5 ("Budget") in full.  
* Addendum 2.15: the sentence that a projected pass of the unit's cost ceiling returns to the Product Owner.  
* Addendum 2.16:  
  * "Cost against the ceiling" and "The unit did not pass its cost ceiling", kept as historical facts of the completed WU-PR-07;  
  * "cost ceiling" among WU-PR-08's unmet prerequisites and among the decisions returned to the Product Owner.  
* Addendum 2.18: the sentence "Its business case and cost ceiling are in the Product Owner decision record §2 (addendum 2.11 rule 1)", read as "Its business case is in the Product Owner decision record §2".

Everything else in those addenda is unchanged. Earlier ceiling decisions and cost-against-ceiling records stay as historical facts with no current effect.

### Epic, task, and status effects

* No status change. No epic, CRD, task, PF09 or PF30 row changes status by this addendum.  
* HDE-CRD-0002 WU-PR-08:  
  * Its business case is the beneficiary, evidence, benefit and measure of docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-WU-PR-08-po-disposition-scope-v1.0.md §2. Its "Cost ceiling" row is not in force.  
  * The ceiling-related text of docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-implementation-plan-change-assessment-v1.1.md is not in force:  
    * in §1, the ceiling bullet;  
    * in P-7, "and the ceiling as the Product Owner sets it" and "The ceiling is the one the Product Owner sets in the recorded decision that §5 asks for";  
    * in §5, "The ceiling question";  
    * in §6, the ceiling row;  
    * in §7, the condition "with the ceiling recorded".  
  * The Plan delta carries no ceiling for WU-PR-08, and IA-30's delta review does not wait on one.  
  * The assessment's corrected fact stands: WU-PR-08 changes the CI classifier, so its pull request runs all seven lanes.  
* HDE-CRD-0002 WU-PR-07: its adopted ceiling (docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-WU-PR-07-po-disposition-business-case-v1.0.md) and the recorded cost against it are historical. The unit is accepted.  
* HDE-CRD-0002 WU-DOC-01 and WU-OPS-01: their business cases, due before DOC-10 and OPS-10, state beneficiary, evidence, benefit and measure only.

### Required canon drainage

None of its own. Permanent PF canon states no cost ceiling for requirements or work units (searched; see Evidence anchors). Addendum 2.11's drainage targets, below, carry the business case without a ceiling:

* HDE Governance §9.7.7;  
* Plan Templates "Business Case (MUST)";  
* HDE CRD Records §4.1.

### Deferred obligations and unresolved work

* Prompt wording changes. Addendum 2.11's prompt changes are unperformed and held in the GCFPE Modification Backlog. When made, they omit every ceiling clause:  
  * IA-30 "and ceiling";  
  * PR-10 "and ceiling";  
  * PR-20 "stays within the ceiling";  
  * PR-30 and PR-35 "record spend against the ceiling";  
  * PR-40 "actual cost against its ceiling".  
* Owner: GCFPE-MGMT-10 and the Product Owner.  
* PF10 numbering and publication: the Product Owner. This record follows addendum 2.18, which is pending publication.

### Scope boundaries and nonclaims

* This addendum changes no prompt, skill, AGENTS.md or permanent PF document. It edits no in-flight record. The records named above are read under it.  
* It is not a QA verdict, acceptance, closure, Plan approval or PR Proceed.

### Evidence anchors

* Direction: the Product Owner's direction, quoted above, given on 2026-10-21 in session [https\://claude.ai/code/session\_012tPq26JWVhCYZmcqTidpV2](https://claude.ai/code/session_012tPq26JWVhCYZmcqTidpV2).  
* Canon searched on origin/main a78b3509: "ceiling" across docs/pfcanon/.  
  * HDE Build Notes: addenda 2.11 to 2.16, each read at its ceiling passages; addenda 2.11 and 2.14 read in full at their rules.  
  * Other hits are unrelated:  
    * HDE Governance (retry delay exp\_ceiling\_ms);  
    * HDE CLI/API/Vendor Reference (retry ceiling\_ms);  
    * HDE Mechanics Guide (a vendor request ceiling);  
    * Glow QA Guide (a review file ceiling).  
  * Read: HDE Build Notes front matter §5 "Individual-addendum supersession" and §9 "Operational rule for agents".  
* In-flight HDE-CRD-0002 records naming a ceiling (searched in docs/ephemeral/): the WU-PR-06 postmortem v1.0 and v1.1, the WU-PR-07 business-case record and its PR records, the WU-PR-08 scope decision record, the addendum 2.18 record, and the Plan change assessment v1.0 and v1.1.  
* Notion: the GCFPE Modification Backlog carries the unperformed prompt changes, including "Record spend against the unit's ceiling in each checkpoint". The 100926.1 prompts the search returned (IA-30, PR-10, RS-20) were last edited on 2026-10-12, before addendum 2.11. The other prompts were not opened.

### Relationship to existing HDE Build Notes guidance

This addendum narrows addendum 2.11 by removing its cost ceiling, and supersedes the ceiling passages of addenda 2.12 to 2.16 and 2.18 listed above. It keeps the rest of addendum 2.11 and addendum 2.14, which remain authoritative for their distinct scopes.

## 2.19 HDE-CRD-0002: Implementation Plan delta 01 is approved — no S01 or version cut, WU-PR-08 is S16 and S17 with one CI classifier registration, and WU-OPS-01 proves deployment by release\_id

Timestamp: 102126 20:50 (UTC). Details: Records the IA-30 approval of the first material delta to the approved HDE-CRD-0002 Implementation Plan v1.1. The delta brings the Plan into line with Specification delta 01 and with two Product Owner directions of 2026-10-21. S01 and its packaging work leave the Plan.     * the loopback bind statements (addenda 2.7, 2.8 and 2.9);  
    * OBS-P4-5, O-19 and the production route exposure, contract F-3 (addenda 2.9 and 2.10).  
  * Not carried: addendum 2.10's "cuts 1.4.0 from release\_id 77204764… (contract F-1)". The Specification delta 01 addendum approves no version cut and governs that overlapping scope (HDE Build Notes front matter §9, step 8: "apply only the highest-numbered applicable addendum to the overlapping scope"; noted in the IA-40 return record §4).  
* Deliverables.  
  * .env.example: a GEO\_API\_KEY entry only.  
  * docs/ADAPTER\_009.md: the readiness rules and port statements.  
  * ci/checks/classify\_ci\_changes.py: one registration that classifies .env.example. The author states its form at PR-20, choosing the smallest repository-grounded registration among the classifier's existing mechanisms.  
  * The registration's owner or pin test, only if one enumerates the registry the registration changes. The author states which, read from the repository.  
  * Repository facts for PR-20 (observed on main 7b763db, identical to a78b350 for these files; PR-20 re-reads them at its head and chooses):  
    * The classifier's existing mechanism for a file that no code reads is the \_DOCUMENT\_PATHS set (LICENSE, AcceptanceMap.md, AGENTS.md, ARCHITECTURE.md, CHANGELOG.md, README.md). A path in it classifies as documentation\_only with no lanes.  
    * Nothing on main reads .env.example: no .py, .sh, .yml, .toml, .json, requirements\*.txt or Procfile reference, and no dotenv loader. The one test hit is a literal in tests/qa/test\_generate\_epic\_close\_pack.py line 4338 (S16's acceptance text in a fixture). So documentation\_only would not hide an affected change (Plan Templates, "CI lane classification and automated budget-control discipline").  
    * No test enumerates \_DOCUMENT\_PATHS (searched tests/, ci/ and tools/). The classifier's scenario table in tests/evidence/test\_rails\_ci\_workflow\_integration.py (test\_change\_classifier\_selects\_expected\_execution\_scenarios) pins example paths, not the set.  
    * The other mechanism that fits is \_WORKFLOW\_CONTROL\_PATH\_LANES, a path-to-lanes map. It would select lanes for every future change to the template.  
* Dependencies and order.  
  * Eighth code unit, after WU-PR-07; the order is kept.  
  * No release member changes, so there is no manifest refresh. ci/checks/classify\_ci\_changes.py, .env.example and docs/ADAPTER\_009.md are not members of release 1.3.0 (45 members, release\_id 77204764…).  
  * A change to the classifier is a full-validation path, so the PR runs all seven lanes.  
  * Lane selection, read from the classifier. ci/checks/classify\_ci\_changes.py is in \_FULL\_VALIDATION\_PATHS. In a throwaway clone of a78b350, one commit changed the three files: it registered .env.example in \_DOCUMENT\_PATHS, added GEO\_API\_KEY and corrected one port line. The classifier, called as ci.yml calls it with full SHAs, then exited 0 with reason=selected\_lanes and all seven lanes (product, compat, db, rails, evidence, qa, release), and wrote 166 changed-test targets. With the registration, .env.example alone classified as documentation\_only.  
* Acceptance and verification.  
  * .env.example has a GEO\_API\_KEY entry and nothing else in it changed.  
  * The readiness section describes /internal/readyz as implemented and names the app that serves it. The port statements match the runner and Procfile bindings.  
  * Admission at the PR head reports 1.3.0, 45 members and the base head's release\_id, unchanged.  
  * At the PR head, the classifier run on .env.example alone returns a classification and no CI\_CHANGE\_SURFACE\_UNCLASSIFIED.  
  * All seven lanes are green on the exact PR head, with CI\_APPLICABILITY\_AND\_EXACT\_HEAD\_OK.  
* Evidence obligations. PR result record.  
* Risks and recovery.  
  * Misclassification. If a later change made code read .env.example, a documentation\_only registration would skip its lanes. PR-20 re-checks the readers at its head, and the registration's form records why it is safe.  
  * The full run exposes failures that are not this unit's. A classifier change runs every lane. A failure that is also red on the base is handled under PR-35's base-red rule, not by widening this unit.  
  * Document drift. S17's statements are checked against adapter/wsgi.py, adapter/factory.py, Procfile and the canonical runner at the PR head. PR-40 checks the measure on the merged head.  
  * R-2 and R-6 no longer apply. Recovery is as the common §7 terms state.  
* Nonclaims. No release cut, no CI workflow change, no QA verdict.  
* Business case. Carried by reference for PR-10 under addendum 2.11 rule 3, as the no-ceiling addendum narrows it: beneficiary, evidence, benefit and measure from docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-WU-PR-08-po-disposition-scope-v1.0.md §2. No cost ceiling applies, and the decision record's "Cost ceiling" row is not in force (no-ceiling addendum, rule 1 and "Epic, task, and status effects"). The corrected fact the change assessment §5 records stands: the unit changes the classifier, so its PR runs all seven lanes.

#### P-9 — §7 WU-OPS-01: Intent, Success criteria, Constraints (revised by D-1 to D-3)

* Intent.  
  The Railway HD Engine production service runs the exact accepted candidate (the main head after WU-PR-08 and WU-DOC-01 merge) so that the QA Plan's bounded open-rails live steps (S02, S10 refusal in the deployed target; S14's live vendor call from the operator shell against the production rails) exercise that candidate. The candidate reaches the service when its commit lands on main (Product Owner direction of 2026-10-21, recorded in docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-implementation-plan-delta-01-review-v1.0.md §2).  
* Success criteria.  
  GET /internal/version on the base URL reports the candidate's identity (the candidate's release\_id at version 1.3.0), which proves the candidate is deployed; the presence-only binding probe; all captured under audit/ops/hde-crd-0002/wu-ops-01/ with path proofs (Plan Templates §3 evidence posture; lower-case directory names).  
* Constraints.  
  No production configuration change by this task. The task does not discover or record the deployment trigger, build command or start command. Rollback intent: redeploy the previously deployed commit.  
  When the candidate's identity does not appear (author statement, D-3). If GET /internal/version does not report the candidate's release\_id once the candidate's commit has landed on main, the task reads it again after the service has had time to deploy. It does not inspect the deploy pipeline or the service settings.  
  * If a later read reports the candidate's release\_id, the success criterion is met.  
  * Otherwise the task records each read: its time, its HTTP status, and either the release\_id the endpoint reported or that the service did not answer. No secret value or raw body is recorded (Secret posture). The task changes no production configuration and stops.  
  * If the service did not answer at all, the task also redeploys the previously deployed commit under the rollback intent, and records that it did.  
  * The Product Owner decides any next step.  
* The task judges deployment by release\_id alone. /internal/version's build\_commit is a fixed value written in the source (engine/runtime/identity.py, \_STATIC\_IDENTITY, "9479d28"), so it does not identify the running commit.

The other WU-OPS-01 fields are unchanged.

### Facts behind the decision

* The CI classifier refuses .env.example: classify\_paths(\['.env.example'\]) raises CI\_CHANGE\_SURFACE\_UNCLASSIFIED:.env.example. ci/checks/classify\_ci\_changes.py is a full-validation path, so a pull request that registers .env.example runs all seven lanes (product, compat, db, rails, evidence, qa, release). docs/ADAPTER\_009.md alone classifies as documentation\_only. Measured on main at bb263fc and 397fca9.  
* No code reads .env.example. The other non-documentation files naming it are a fixture literal in tests/qa/test\_generate\_epic\_close\_pack.py and historical hash lists and notes that no code reads. No test names \_DOCUMENT\_PATHS outside the classifier.  
* .env.example, docs/ADAPTER\_009.md and ci/checks/classify\_ci\_changes.py are not members of release 1.3.0 (45 members, release\_id 77204764c225b6458e748d8ba6ca2b196dd741b88a7250221a94a1640666c51e), so WU-PR-08 makes no manifest refresh.  
* /internal/version reports release\_id derived from catalog/manifest.json; its build\_commit is a fixed source value (engine/runtime/identity.py, \_STATIC\_IDENTITY, "9479d28") and does not identify the running commit.  
* No PF document states the HD Engine service's deploy source. Glow Infrastructure records the live start setting as unverified (§10) and the build artifact as TBD (§4.1), and states that PO-provided deployed environment facts must not be downgraded to OPEN/TBD (§2.4).

### Affected surfaces, dependencies and consequences

* Requirements: S01 leaves the Plan and is not delivered. S16 and S17 stay in WU-PR-08. No other requirement's unit, scope or acceptance changes.  
* WU-PR-08: S16, S17 and one classifier registration for .env.example; its pull request runs all seven lanes; no manifest refresh; no CI workflow change. The registration's form is chosen at its PR-20 from the classifier's existing mechanisms. Its business case is the beneficiary, evidence, benefit and measure of the WU-PR-08 decision record §2, with no cost ceiling.  
* WU-DOC-01: drops the dedicated-environment install note; states release 1.3.0, refreshed in place with no version cut, and its current release\_id.  
* WU-OPS-01: the candidate reaches the service when its commit lands on main. GET /internal/version reporting the candidate's release\_id at version 1.3.0 proves deployment. The unit records no deployment trigger, build command or start command, deploys nothing itself, and redeploys the previous commit only under its rollback intent.  
* Release identity: version 1.3.0, build time 2026-08-24T18:04:49Z, 45 members, unchanged admission constants. release\_id moves only by a same-version refresh in a unit that changes a member; no remaining unit is planned to.  
* Tests: no wheel-install test and no pinned-identity rewrite to 1.4.0.  
* Risks: R-2 and R-8 cover WU-PR-03/04/05 only; R-6 and R-9 are removed; WU-PR-08 carries its own risks (misclassification, failures a full run exposes that are not the unit's, document drift).  
* QA: the QA Plan needs no Live QA step or exemption for S01.

### Exclusions and nonclaims

* No unit added, removed or reordered; no interface added; no release version cut; no admission-constant change; no CI workflow change; no cost ceiling for any unit.  
* Not delivered: a regular wheel install outside a source checkout still does not admit the release (Specification delta 01, C-6).  
* This approval is not a PR Instruction, PR Proceed, Ops completion, deployment, QA verdict, acceptance or closure, and moves no PF09 or PF30 row.

### Conflicts

* Addendum 2.10 carries "WU-PR-08 (S17) cuts 1.4.0 … (contract F-1)"; addendum 2.6 states that WU-PR-08's version bump regenerates the engine-core purity report. The Specification delta 01 addendum approves no version cut and governs that overlapping scope. Neither statement applies to WU-PR-08; 2.10's other WU-PR-08 items (OBS-P4-5, O-19, F-3) and the loopback bind statements of 2.7 to 2.9 stay carried.  
* Change assessment v1.1 and the WU-PR-08 decision record's "Cost ceiling" row carry a ceiling; addendum 2.18 takes them out of force.  
* Change assessment v1.1 P-9 required WU-OPS-01 to record the live start command, and the Specification delta 01 addendum assigned "WU-OPS-01 records the deployment facts; Product Owner" as an unresolved obligation. The Product Owner's deployment direction supersedes P-9's start-command parts and discharges that obligation. The Specification text, including C-6's stated limit, is unchanged.  
* The Specification delta 01 addendum's record and addendum 2.18 both carry the number 2.18. The delta and this addendum cite the former by title and record; no decision depends on its number.

### Unresolved obligations and owners

* Business cases for WU-DOC-01 and WU-OPS-01 (beneficiary, evidence, benefit, measure), due before DOC-10 and OPS-10: Product Owner.  
* Glow Infrastructure doc-delta candidate: the HD Engine deploy source (commits landing on main of amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2), §3.1 and §4.1: Product Owner.  
* The PF10 number of the Specification delta 01 addendum, and the correction of addendum 2.11's internal cross-references: Product Owner.  
* The registration's form: WU-PR-08's PR-20.

### Return phase

The continuation is PR-10 — Create PR Work-Unit Instructions — 100926.1, for WU-PR-08, from Plan v1.1 plus this delta.

### Relationship to existing HDE Build Notes guidance

This addendum applies the Specification delta 01 addendum and addendum 2.18 to the Implementation Plan, and records the Product Owner's deployment direction for HDE-CRD-0002. For WU-PR-08 it narrows the items addenda 2.6 and 2.10 carried, as stated under Conflicts. It supersedes no other addendum.

## 2.20 HDE-CRD-0002: Specification delta 01 is approved — requirement S01 is removed and the release keeps its version, with a same-version manifest refresh for any member change

Timestamp: 102126 18:36 (UTC). Details: Records the CF-C-30 approval of the first Specification delta to the approved HDE-CRD-0002 CRD Specification. The delta removes requirement S01 (every release member packaged with the distribution), drops any release version cut so the release stays at 1.3.0, and replaces the Specification's "manifest re-cut and new admission pins" constraint with a same-version manifest refresh. S16 and S17 are kept. The approved base is unchanged; the base plus this delta is the current Specification.

### Facts behind the decision

* Production starts the engine from the checkout: Procfile reads web: /app/.venv/bin/python \-m gunicorn 'adapter.factory:create\_app()' …, and \-m places the working directory first on the import path. The live Railway start setting is unverified (Glow Infrastructure §10); if production ran without the checkout, admission would refuse to start (MISSING\_FILE).

## 2.22 HDE-CRD-0002 WU-OPS-01: the Ops task receipt v1.0 accepts the production release-identity read and the presence of seven variables, and the next stage is QA-10

Details: Records the OPS-30 review (Ops task receipt v1.0) of Ops task WU-OPS-01 v1.0 and its execution result v1.0 (attempt 1, the only attempt) of the approved HDE-CRD-0002 Implementation Plan v1.1 as amended by Plan delta 01 v1.1 (P-9, P-18, P-19, P-20): decision ACCEPT. ## 2.23 HDE-CRD-0002: remediation of QA-10 finding FND-001 is approved as changed — the evidence-lane tests stop assuming the change's own QA root and close pack are absent, through new work unit WU-REM-01

Timestamp: 102326 16:40 (UTC). Details: Records the ESC-40 approval, with one change, of Thoth's remediation proposal v1.0 for the HDE-CRD-0002 QA-10 finding FND-001.

* The finding. Requirement S22's QA evidence admission is not achievable on the real repository, because six tests in tests/evidence/test\_evidence\_index\_missing\_state.py, which the evidence lane runs, assume that audit/qa/hde-crd-0002/ and the change's close pack are absent. Publishing HDE-CRD-0002's QA evidence would fail that lane.  
* The remedy. It is test-only and delivered by a new work unit, WU-REM-01. One Plan acceptance clause is restated.  
* What is unchanged. The approved Plan v1.1 and the Specification. WU-PR-02 stays accepted-final.

### Source and approval

* Producing prompt: ESC-40 — Review and Return Approved Remediation — 100926.1, release GCFPE-20261009.1 / 100926.1.  
* Decision: APPROVE\_AS\_CHANGED by Isis, the continuing HDE-CRD-0002 Lead Developer, recorded in docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-remediation-review-FND-001-v1.0.md, 2026-10-23.  
* Reviewed proposal: docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-remediation-proposal-FND-001-v1.0.md (Thoth, ESC-30; file SHA-256 d120223b8e9d5f51176228153a1debf8274f142cd780520cf7513c0a4d02b70d; landed by [\#707](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/707)).  
* Originating finding: QA-10 readiness v1.0, NOT\_READY (docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-QA10-qa-readiness-v1.0.md); triage §3 FND-001 (docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-QA10-change-audit-triage-v1.0.md; [\#706](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/706)).  
* Immutable base: Implementation Plan v1.1 (IA-30 review v1.1, INITIAL\_APPROVE), with Plan delta 01 v1.1 (addendum 2.19). The base file is not edited.

### Cause

* The three loci, all in tests/evidence/test\_evidence\_index\_missing\_state.py:  
  * test\_absent\_admitted\_crd\_root\_publishes\_nothing asserts that the real checkout holds no audit/qa/hde-crd-0002/, no loader row and no Index row.  
  * The crd\_repo fixture strips only audit/qa/hde-crd-0001, so test\_crd\_0002\_fixture\_root\_publishes\_beside\_crd\_0001 sees real HDE-CRD-0002 checks.  
  * The close\_repo fixture asserts that the real checkout holds no audit/qa/hde-crd-0002. The close-pack test it serves also assumes the real close pack is absent.  
* Why the test exists. WU-PR-02 delivered it to satisfy Plan v1.1 acceptance (5), "the absent real root publishes nothing", which held at delivery.  
* Reproduction. In a throwaway clone of main 7d5a5aa with a stand-in HDE-CRD-0002 family published by the canonical updater:  
  * the evidence-lane pytest files gave 2 failed and 4 errors, against 134 passed without the root;  
  * the seven evidence validators, the QA test files and the release lane stayed green.  
* What is not at fault. The updater, the harness and the release lane work with the root present.

### Approved remediation

* P-1, Implementation Plan v1.1 §7 WU-PR-02, "Acceptance and verification" item (5), last clause. "the absent real root publishes nothing" reads: "an absent admitted HDE-CRD-0002 root publishes nothing, proven in a copy of the checkout from which that root and its Index rows are removed; the evidence-lane tests do not depend on whether the real checkout holds the HDE-CRD-0002 QA root, its close-run family, its close pack or its close candidate source". WU-PR-02's accepted delivery is unchanged; the new proof is delivered by WU-REM-01.  
* P-2, a new work unit in Plan v1.1 §7, after WU-OPS-01 and before QA-10 readiness: WU-REM-01 — Evidence-lane independence from the HDE-CRD-0002 QA root (PR; Distillation).  
  * Purpose: let HDE-CRD-0002's QA evidence and close pack land without breaking the evidence lane.  
  * Bounded scope: S22, and S21's close-run tests; tests/evidence/test\_evidence\_index\_missing\_state.py only.  
  * Dependencies: none on unmerged work; it lands before QA-20.  
  * Acceptance: V-1 to V-5.  
  * Evidence obligations: the PR result record with the V-2 and V-3 rehearsal outputs; no governed evidence change.  
  * Business case:  
    * beneficiary: QA and the Product Owner;  
    * evidence: the reproduction above;  
    * benefit: QA-20 and QA-50 can proceed, and the first QA evidence and close-pack pull requests are not blocked by the evidence lane;  
    * measure: the first pull request that publishes HDE-CRD-0002 QA evidence passes the evidence lane with no change to these tests.  
  * Nonclaims: no HDE-CRD-0002 QA evidence is produced or committed.  
* Required behaviour in that file:  
  * R-1: the absence test builds the absent state in a copy, removing the root and its Index rows and regenerating and checking through the canonical updater. It then asserts no loader row and no Index row under the root. It does not read the real checkout's state.  
  * R-2: crd\_repo builds the pre-first-publication state for every root in ADMITTED\_CRD\_QA, and the existing assertions stay.  
  * R-3: close\_repo removes audit/qa/hde-crd-0002/ (including close/), audit/HDE-CRD-0002\_close\_report.md, audit/HDE-CRD-0002\_MANIFEST.json and audit/HDE-CRD-0002\_close\_candidate\_source.json, with their path proofs and Index rows, then regenerates and checks through the canonical updater. The four close-run tests keep their assertions.  
  * No other file changes.  
* Verification:  
  * V-1: on the PR head, the seven evidence-lane validators exit 0, and the six evidence-lane pytest files pass with no fewer than 134 tests.  
  * V-2: in a throwaway clone with a stand-in HDE-CRD-0002 QA family published through the harness and canonical updater, the evidence and release lanes pass as ci.yml runs them.  
  * V-3: the same clone additionally holds a stand-in close-run family, the two close-pack files and the close candidate source, indexed by the canonical updater; the evidence lane passes.  
  * V-4: the R-1 test fails when an Index row under the root is planted in its copy.  
  * V-5: hosted CI on the PR head has the evidence lane green and ends CI\_APPLICABILITY\_AND\_EXACT\_HEAD\_OK.  
  * Nothing from a rehearsal clone is committed.  
* Change C-1 from the proposal: the close candidate source (audit/HDE-CRD-0002\_close\_candidate\_source.json, the path the close-pack tests name) is added to R-3 and V-3.

### Affected surfaces and consequences

* Requirements: S22 becomes achievable on the real root; S21's close-run tests stop depending on the real checkout. No other requirement is affected.  
* Tests: one file. CI: the evidence lane only. Release identity, Ops and documentation: unchanged.  
* Order: WU-REM-01 is delivered and accepted before the HDE-CRD-0002 QA-10 reassessment, and QA-20 waits on that reassessment.

### Exclusions and nonclaims

* No runtime, updater, harness or governed-evidence change.  
* No new evidence family, no Specification change, and no re-run of an accepted-final pull request.  
* This approval is not a PR Instruction, Proceed, merge, QA verdict, acceptance or closure, and it moves no PF09 or PF30 row.

### Conflicts

None. The canon-conflict register is unchanged: CR-1 to CR-8 are decided, and CR-9 is proposed and unaffected.

* The QA-10 reassessment of S22 and its dependants, after WU-REM-01 is accepted: Isis.  
* CR-9's disposition: Isis, at its native review boundary.

### Relationship to existing HDE Build Notes guidance

## 2.24 HDE-CRD-0002: the FND-001 remedy is amended — WU-REM-01 also makes the Mirror skeleton test and the QA harness accept crd\_id, so HDE-CRD-0002's close pack leaves the evidence lane, the qa lane and Epic viability as they were

Timestamp: 102326 20:15 (UTC). Details: Records the ESC-40 approval of Thoth's remediation proposal v1.1 for the HDE-CRD-0002 QA-10 finding FND-001 (requirement S22). It amends the remedy addendum 2.23 recorded.  
The planner of work unit WU-REM-01 found (F-1) that the approved one-file remedy could not meet its own acceptance. Diagnosis then found a second defect with the same cause (F-2). The two defects:

* F-1. Once HDE-CRD-0002's close pack is indexed, tests/evidence/test\_evidence\_skeleton.py::test\_mirror\_schema\_and\_parity rejects the crd\_id key the canonical updater writes on its two close-pack rows.  
* F-2. The QA harness (tools/qa/qa\_harness.py) rejects crd\_id on Human Index and Machine Mirror rows. With the close pack indexed, four Epic acceptance-map viabilities (HDE-EPIC021, 027, 028, 029\) turn from PASS to FAIL\_TOOLING, and the qa-lane test test\_current\_acceptance\_inputs\_are\_strictly\_viable fails.

WU-REM-01 widens to three files: R-4 and R-5 add crd\_id to the allowed optional keys of those two validators. The rest of the 2.23 remedy stands.

### Source and approval

* Producing prompt: ESC-40 — Review and Return Approved Remediation — 100926.1; release GCFPE-20261009.1 / 100926.1.  
* Decision: APPROVE by Isis, the continuing HDE-CRD-0002 Lead Developer, recorded in docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-remediation-review-FND-001-v1.1.md, 2026-10-23.  
* Reviewed proposal: docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-remediation-proposal-FND-001-v1.1.md (Thoth, ESC-30; file SHA-256 2aa11d33e3b0cd0332aa0471584e3b0662dc5ab8f796bbf2c9c5fe6d7e07474f; [\#711](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/711)). Predecessor proposal v1.0 ([\#707](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/707)) and review v1.0 ([\#708](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/708)), recorded by addendum 2.23.  
* Delivery-stage evidence: WU-REM-01 PR implementation plan v1.0 §12 and §16, BLOCKED on F-1 ([\#710](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/710)); instruction v1.0 ([\#709](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/709)) §14 routes a change to the remedy through ESC-30 and ESC-40.  
* Immutable base: Implementation Plan v1.1 with Plan delta 01 v1.1. Not edited.

### Cause

* HDE Schemas and Artifacts §8.3, "Allowed top-level keys and unknown-key rejection", lists crd\_id as an optional metadata key and requires that "every serializer or validator enforcing this allowed-key set MUST accept crd\_id".  
* The canonical updater and ci/checks/check\_mirror\_schema.sh accept it. The Mirror skeleton test and the QA harness do not.  
* The updater writes crd\_id only on CRD close-pack rows. So the defects appear when the close pack is indexed, not when the QA root alone exists.  
* The close-pack pull request selects only the evidence and release lanes. F-2 would first fail on a later, unrelated qa-lane run.  
* All of this was reproduced in throwaway clones of main with stand-in states produced only through the harness and the canonical updater.

### Approved remediation

* P-1, unchanged from addendum 2.23: Implementation Plan v1.1 §7 WU-PR-02, "Acceptance and verification" item (5), last clause, reads: "an absent admitted HDE-CRD-0002 root publishes nothing, proven in a copy of the checkout from which that root and its Index rows are removed; the evidence-lane tests do not depend on whether the real checkout holds the HDE-CRD-0002 QA root, its close-run family, its close pack or its close candidate source".  
* P-2, amended. WU-REM-01 — Evidence-lane independence from the HDE-CRD-0002 QA root (PR; Distillation):  
  * Purpose: let HDE-CRD-0002's QA evidence and close pack land without breaking the evidence lane, and keep the qa lane and existing Epic acceptance-map viability unaffected once the close pack is on main.  
  * Bounded scope: S22, and S21's close-run tests and close-pack rows. Three files: tests/evidence/test\_evidence\_index\_missing\_state.py, tests/evidence/test\_evidence\_skeleton.py and tools/qa/qa\_harness.py.  
  * Dependencies: none on unmerged work; it lands before QA-20.  
  * Acceptance: V-1 to V-5.  
  * Evidence obligations: the PR result record with the V-2 and V-3 rehearsal outputs, the V-3 viability comparison included; no governed-evidence change.  
  * Nonclaims: no HDE-CRD-0002 QA evidence, close pack or close candidate source is produced or committed.  
* Required behaviour:  
  * R-1, unchanged: the absence test builds the absent state in a copy, through the canonical updater, and does not read the real checkout's state.  
  * R-2, unchanged: crd\_repo builds the pre-first-publication state for every root in ADMITTED\_CRD\_QA.  
  * R-3 with C-1, unchanged: close\_repo removes audit/qa/hde-crd-0002/ (including close/), audit/HDE-CRD-0002\_close\_report.md, audit/HDE-CRD-0002\_MANIFEST.json and audit/HDE-CRD-0002\_close\_candidate\_source.json, with their path proofs and Index rows, from the copy, then regenerates and checks through the canonical updater.  
  * R-4, new: test\_mirror\_schema\_and\_parity accepts crd\_id as an optional top-level Mirror key. Nothing else in that file changes.  
  * R-5, new: the harness's evidence-graph validation accepts crd\_id as an optional key on Human Index and Machine Mirror rows. The required keys, the other metadata checks, the strict Epic predicates and the refusal of CRD viability are unchanged.  
  * Limit: no file outside the three changes.  
* Verification:  
  * V-1: on the PR head, the seven evidence-lane validators exit 0; the six evidence-lane pytest files pass with no fewer than 134 tests; the qa lane's seven files pass with its two tree checks; and the classifier's changed-test targets pass in isolation, all as ci.yml runs them.  
  * V-2: in a throwaway clone with a stand-in HDE-CRD-0002 QA family published by the harness and canonical updater, the evidence, qa and release lanes pass.  
  * V-3: the same clone additionally holds a stand-in close-run family, the two close-pack files and the close candidate source, indexed by the canonical updater. (a) The evidence, qa and release lanes pass. (b) Each docs/acceptance\_map\_epic0NN.json gets the same evaluate\_acceptance\_map\_viability status as in the V-2 state, and no result names crd\_id.  
  * V-4, unchanged: the R-1 test fails when an Index row under the root is planted in its copy.  
  * V-5: on hosted CI on the PR head, every selected lane and the changed-test step are green, and the run ends CI\_APPLICABILITY\_AND\_EXACT\_HEAD\_OK.  
  * Nothing from a rehearsal clone is committed.  
* Business case:  
  * beneficiary: QA and the Product Owner; every later qa-lane pull request; users of the harness's Epic viability;  
  * evidence: the reproductions above;  
  * benefit: QA-20 and QA-50 can proceed, and the close pack leaves no lane or Epic viability failing;  
  * measure (1): the first pull request that publishes HDE-CRD-0002 QA evidence passes the evidence lane with no change to these tests;  
  * measure (2): the close-pack pull request passes the evidence lane, and the first qa-lane run on main after it passes, with no change to these three files. Neither measure is observable at WU-REM-01's PR-40; V-2 and V-3 are the proxy.  
  * No cost ceiling.

### Conflict decided: CR-10

* The conflict. HDE Schemas and Artifacts §8.3 also asks every validator of the key set to "enforce the epic\_id/crd\_id mutual-exclusion rule". A row with both keys cannot reach the skeleton test or the harness: the sole writer refuses it, and check\_mirror\_schema.sh rejects it (EPIC\_CRD\_EXCLUSIVE) on every evidence-lane run.  
* Decision: APPROVED, alternative (a). Addendum 2.11 rule 5, kept by addendum 2.18 rule 6, governs for this exact scope: such a row is an input outside the real set for these two validators, and it gets a stated limit, not a model.  
  * The mutual-exclusion rule stays enforced by the canonical writer and the Machine Mirror schema check.  
  * R-4 and R-5 accept crd\_id only.  
* Drainage: HDE Schemas and Artifacts §8.3, to state where the mutual-exclusion rule is enforced. Unperformed.

### Affected surfaces and consequences

* Requirements:  
  * S22 becomes achievable on the real root.  
  * S21's close-run tests stop depending on the real checkout, and its close-pack rows stop failing a validator.  
  * S22's "existing Epic … inputs still publish and validate" is preserved once the close pack lands.  
* Files: two test files and one QA tool. None is a release member; release identity is unchanged.  
* CI: WU-REM-01's pull request runs the evidence and qa lanes and the changed-test step.  
* Ops and documentation: none.  
* Order: WU-REM-01 is delivered and accepted before the HDE-CRD-0002 QA-10 reassessment, and QA-20 waits on that reassessment.  
* This addendum supersedes addendum 2.23's WU-REM-01 scope, limit, exclusions, verification and business case. 2.23's P-1 and R-1 to R-3 with C-1 stand unchanged.

### Exclusions and nonclaims

* No updater or runtime change, and no harness change beyond R-5.  
* No regeneration or hand edit of governed evidence, and no new evidence family.  
* No Specification change.  
* No reopening of WU-PR-02 or WU-PR-07, and no re-run of an accepted-final pull request.  
* The 12 Epic acceptance maps that already fail viability on main, for reasons unrelated to crd\_id, are not repaired.  
* This approval is not a PR Instruction, Proceed, merge, QA verdict, acceptance or closure, and it moves no PF09 or PF30 row.

### Unresolved obligations and owners

* WU-REM-01 instruction v1.1: the whole-change Implementation Agent. The successor PR plan: WU-REM-01's PR-20.  
* The QA-10 reassessment of S22 and its dependants, after WU-REM-01 is accepted: Isis. It includes one observation: 9 local failures of tests/evidence/test\_hdapi\_v2\_contract\_inventory.py in the remediation author's environment, a file that hosted full-validation run 39699657729 passed.  
* CR-10 drainage, and the 12 Epic viabilities already failing: the Product Owner.  
* CR-9's disposition: Isis, at its native review boundary.

### Relationship to existing HDE Build Notes guidance

This addendum amends addendum 2.23 for WU-REM-01 within the scope stated above. It applies addendum 2.11 rule 5 to HDE Schemas and Artifacts §8.3's mutual-exclusion duty for two validators (CR-10). It leaves addenda 2.5 and 2.16, which accepted WU-PR-02 and WU-PR-07, standing.
