## 2.27 HDE-CRD-0002: remediation of QA-110 finding QA110-F01 is approved as changed — the pre-push check's throwaway copies own their objects, so a full-history checkout stays unchanged, through new work unit WU-REM-02

Timestamp: 102526 03:54 (UTC). Details: Records the ESC-40 approval, as changed, of Thoth's remediation proposal v1.0 for the HDE-CRD-0002 QA-110 finding QA110-F01 (requirement S20; QA Plan v1.2 check 13 qa-13-prepush-check, task T13, attempt 1). The delivered read-only pre-push check, tools/ci/prepush\_check.py, exits 4 (PREPUSH\_CHECK\_READ\_ONLY\_VIOLATION) on a full-history checkout although every report passes. The remedy changes how the tool makes its throwaway copies, names the changed entries when the guard trips, and adds regression tests. A new routed work unit, WU-REM-02, delivers it. WU-PR-06 stays accepted-final (addendum 2.12).

### Source and approval

* Producing prompt: ESC-40 — Review and Return Approved Remediation — 100926.1; release GCFPE-20261009.1 / 100926.1.  
* Decision: APPROVE\_AS\_CHANGED by Isis, the continuing HDE-CRD-0002 Lead Developer, recorded in docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-remediation-review-QA110-F01-v1.0.md, 2026-10-25. One change, C-1, corrects the proposal's return route.  
* Reviewed proposal: docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-remediation-proposal-QA110-F01-v1.0.md (Thoth, ESC-30; file SHA-256 6b3b4945591e6d309bde1006dbc02e725cbe4e5b74bba77934dbc9c2ff3fc470; [\#730](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/730)).  
* Originating finding: docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-ESC10-qa-escalation-report-v1.0.md ([\#729](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/729)), from docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-QA110-qa-evidence-review-v1.0.md §6 ([\#728](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/728)). Evidence of record: audit/qa/hde-crd-0002/checks/qa-13-prepush-check/primary.log.  
* Immutable bases: as the front matter lists. Not edited.

### Cause

* make\_copy in tools/ci/prepush\_check.py makes every throwaway copy with git clone \--shared. Such a copy borrows the source checkout's object store through .git/objects/info/alternates.  
* When git writes an object that already exists in a store it reaches, it writes nothing and instead updates the timestamps (mtime and ctime) of the file holding the object (a "freshen"). Through the borrowed store, that file is the source checkout's pack.  
* In replay step 08, the tool's own owner test tests/tools/test\_prepush\_check.py commits in \--shared fixture clones of the replay copy. Those commits freshen the source's pack. The guard, which compares the lstat identity of every file under the source's .git, correctly reports the change.  
* Git version is not the factor. A shallow source disables \--shared, so the copy gets its own packs: the earlier passing runs used shallow sources. QA Plan v1.2's E-L is a full-history clone, the operator's real environment, and engages the mechanism.  
* A timestamp change to a file under the checkout's .git, made by code the tool runs, is a write to the repository under the WU-PR-06 instruction's ID-2 reading of S20 ("No report may create, modify or delete any file there"). The defect is in the copy mechanism. The guard's precision is correct and stays.

### Approved delta

* R-1. The copy owns its objects. make\_copy makes every throwaway copy with git clone \--quiet \--no-hardlinks \--dissociate \--no-checkout \-- \<source\> \<destination\> and the detached checkout as before. A copy whose .git/objects/info/alternates exists after the clone is a ToolingError. All copying reports (lanes, membership, sweep, replay) use it.  
* R-2. A violation names what changed. repository\_state() keeps the git-metadata entries by name. A new state\_changes(before, after) lists each differing entry with the fields that moved, capped at 20 lines plus a count. main prints each as PREPUSH\_CHECK\_READ\_ONLY\_VIOLATION: changed: \<line\> on stderr after the existing violation line. Exit status 4, stdout and the result line are unchanged. Paths and field names only; no file content, no environment value.  
* R-3. The guard's precision is unchanged. Every field compared before is compared after, by equality of the whole state.  
* R-4. Regression tests in tests/tools/test\_prepush\_check.py:  
  * (a) a copy of a packed and of a loose source owns its objects, and a write in it of an existing blob leaves the source's state equal; in the same test, a negative control shows a \--shared copy changing the source's metadata only;  
  * (b) a copy of a source that itself borrows objects reaches neither the source nor its base;  
  * (c) state\_changes names a rewritten tracked file and a rewritten .git/config and carries no file content.  
* The existing 137 tests stay as they are.  
* R-5. Documentation. README.md "Pre-push check (HDE-CRD-0002)": the exit-status line says the changed entries are named on stderr, and the Limits list states that the copies own their objects, so a source that borrows objects is dissociated in the copy at the cost of a repack.  
* R-6. File set. Only tools/ci/prepush\_check.py, tests/tools/test\_prepush\_check.py and README.md. No report's output lines, inventory pin, child environment, temporary-directory rule, exit code or classifier registration changes.  
* Work unit. WU-REM-02: PR; Distillation; not production-affecting; S20. It lands after WU-REM-01 and before the QA-120 report. Proposal v1.0 §6.3's prototype is one rehearsed implementation; the WU-REM-02 plan may adopt or improve it within R-1 to R-6.  
* Stated limits. Each copy holds the source's object store by value (about 58 MB for this repository). A borrowing source costs one repack in the copy. A shallow source still gives copies ref-reachable history only (LR6-O7). Concurrent git activity in the checkout is still reported as a violation (IF-35-24), now with the changed entries named.  
* C-1. Return route for check 13\. QA Plan v1.2 is an approved base. Once WU-REM-02 lands, check 13 step 3's literal "137 passed" no longer matches the owner test. Its expected count therefore changes through a standalone bounded delta to the approved QA Plan:  
  * Kronos authors the delta, as the Plan's owner at the return (Glow QA Guide §10.6 step 5).  
  * Isis decides it at QA-70 in APPROVED\_QA\_PLAN\_DELTA\_REVIEW mode.  
  * The delta does not go through QA-80, which revises only pending Plans.  
  * Check 13's objective, target, step 1 and step 2 predicates and outcome map are unchanged.

### Verification

* V-1: the owner test passes on the WU-REM-02 head (141 tests if R-4 lands as specified), in the PR session's clone with CI's install, Python 3.12, ruby, bash, curl, under closed rails.  
* V-2: on a full-history scratch clone of the candidate head, check 13 step 1's command exits 0 with every report PASS and the final PREPUSH\_CHECK\_RESULT: PASS line reporting the repository unchanged; step 2 exits 0; the clone's git status \--porcelain is empty before and after.  
* V-3: a negative control at the command level. With make\_copy reverted to \--shared in the scratch clone only, the same command exits 4 and names the pack's (mtime, ctime).  
* V-4: hosted CI on the pull request is green, including the changed-tests step that runs the registered owner test.  
* V-5: PR-40 confirms the landed blobs equal the reviewed head and records V-1 to V-4.  
* V-6: Kronos verifies check 13 in the QA-100 venue class (E-L, full history) under the Plan as amended by C-1's delta.

Evidence already observed before approval (planning validation, not V-1 to V-6):

* The proposal's rehearsals under git 2.43.0 and 2.53.0.  
* The reviewer's independent runs on full-history clones at main 1dc12b0d (git 2.43.0, CPython 3.12.3, ruby 3.3.6, closed rails):  
  * the delivered tool exited 4, with the source pack's mtime and ctime moved and its size and git status unchanged;  
  * the prototype exited 0 with all five reports PASS, replay step 08 "4527 passed", and "repository unchanged", with the source pack untouched.

### Business case (addendum 2.11 rule 1, as addendum 2.18 narrows it)

* Beneficiary: every PR session and operator who runs the tool on a full-history checkout; Kronos, who can then verify check 13; the HDE-CRD-0002 close, which a FAIL\_BEHAVIOR check blocks (addendum 2.17 resolution A).  
* Evidence: QA110-F01, two executions in attempt 1, reproduced by the proposal and by the review.  
* Benefit: exit 0 with "repository unchanged" on a full-history checkout. When a violation does occur, the tool names its cause.  
* Measure: V-6, and the regression tests green on hosted CI on the pull request and on main. At PR-40, V-1 to V-4 are the proxy and the measure is "not yet measurable" (addendum 2.18 rule 5).  
* This fixes a breach of the read-only guarantee on a real input (addendum 2.11 rules 4(b) and 7). No cost ceiling applies (addendum 2.18).

### Affected surfaces and consequences

* Requirement S20: met on full-history checkouts once delivered; its text and acceptance are unchanged.  
* tools/ci/prepush\_check.py: not a release member; release 1.3.0 and its release\_id are unchanged.  
* tests/tools/test\_prepush\_check.py and README.md: as R-4 and R-5.  
* CI: the classifier's lanes for the three paths. The registered owner test runs in the changed-tests step. No ci.yml change.  
* QA Plan v1.2 check 13: step 3's count changes through C-1's delta. The rest of the check is unchanged.  
* Downstream: check 13's verification, the QA-120 report and the S21 close decision.  
* Not affected: production, the deployed service, release members, vendor and database paths, the evidence graph and PF canon.

### Exclusions and nonclaims

* No change to the guard's fields or equality rule. No change to the owner test's existing \--shared fixtures (observation O-A stays with the Product Owner).  
* No change to ci.yml, the classifier, the sweep definition, the child environment, the temporary-directory rules, exit codes, AGENTS.md or PF canon.  
* Specification S20 is unchanged. The Product Owner decisions D-1/LR6-O1 (whether S20 is removed) and FND-028 (whether replay is removed) are not presumed. If S20 is removed, WU-REM-02 is moot.  
* The OPS03 cross-device failure (O-1) is an independent defect outside S20 and this remedy (Glow QA Guide §3.4.8). It is routed to the Product Owner.  
* This addendum is not QA, acceptance, a merge, deployment or closure. Check 13 stays FAIL\_BEHAVIOR until its verification under the remediation return. The original failure stays visible as failure context (Glow QA Guide §10.6).

### Unresolved obligations and owners

* WU-REM-02 instruction (PR-10) and the delivery lineage through PR-40: the whole-change Implementation Agent, the WU-REM-02 PR session and the Product Owner (Proceed, merge).  
* C-1's QA Plan delta for check 13 step 3: Kronos (author); Isis (QA-70 decision).  
* Check 13's verification: Kronos.  
* O-1 (OPS03 EXDEV) and O-A (the owner test's fixtures freshen the developer's checkout): the Product Owner, as later-change candidates.  
* D-1/LR6-O1 and FND-028: the Product Owner, unchanged.  
* PR35-C1/FND-024: unchanged (addendum 2.21). A verification run red with that signature is FAIL\_TOOLING under check 13's Outcomes.

* RCA: causes and patterns.  
  * Outcome classes: PASS all 21 current statuses; FAIL\_BEHAVIOR check 13 attempt 1 (a product defect with readiness held and QA tooling trustworthy); TOOLING\_BLOCKED check 3 attempt 1 and check 21's first run (readiness reads failed, not behavior results); FAIL\_TOOLING none. Hosted CI and the pre-push tool's result are cited evidence, not QA verdicts.  
  * QA110-F01 cause (evidenced; addendum 2.27 "Cause"). make\_copy made every throwaway copy with git clone \--shared, so each copy borrowed the source checkout's object store. In replay step 08 the owner test commits in \--shared fixture clones of the replay copy; writing an object that already exists in a reachable store freshens the file that holds it, here the source checkout's pack; the guard compares the lstat identity of every file under the source's .git, so it correctly reported the change. The defect was in the copy mechanism; the guard's precision was correct. Earlier passing runs (the WU-PR-06 PR-40 lineage review on fc51620; the QA Audit item 9 rehearsal on 0b15328d) used shallow sources, which disable \--shared; Plan v1.2's E-L is a full-history clone, the operator's real environment. Git's version is not the factor: RV-1 held under git 2.53.0 as under 2.43.0 (O-8). Contributing: the validation venues before QA used shallow sources (hosted CI's checkout depth is not established); the owner test's own \--shared fixtures perform the writes (O-A, unchanged); check 13's first execution also met O-1, which made the first signal noisier. Remedy: WU-REM-02 (copies own their objects, R-1; a violation names what changed, R-2); Plan v1.3 changed check 13 step 3's count to 141 (C-1, through D-1 to D-3, CR-12); RV-1 passed under git 2.53.0 (V-6). Detection: found by the approved QA environment as designed.  
  * Check 3 attempt 1 (TOOLING\_BLOCKED): "No interpreter meets step 1 in this venue: /usr/bin/python3 (3.14.4) makes \_distutils\_system\_mod importable; /usr/bin/python3.13, 3.12 and 3.11 are absent". Cause: collection v1.0's NZ-02 listed only system interpreters, written against an Ubuntu 24.04 container where /usr/bin/python3.13 met E-D; the executor's venue was an Ubuntu 26.04 workstation chosen by the Product Owner (Kronos's collection defect O-4). Correction: the Moon Loop (PO-1), reinstall after rerun 1's rpds.rpds import error; no repository file, predicate or expected value changed. Residual: R-1's caveat; PO-2 is the context.  
  * Check 21's first run (TOOLING\_BLOCKED): readiness (i) printed docs/prompt\_ecosystem\_management/authoritative-surfaces.md \| 6 \+++---, changed in 63335e66 ([\#737](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/737)) after da5735eb; Plan v1.3 §7 rule A maps a non-empty diff to TOOLING\_BLOCKED; published at d639772d (blob 3e1562ea1175). Cause (O-11): Plan v1.3's state-(b) guards exclude only docs/ephemeral and docs/pfcanon, two of the eight CI-exempt storage prefixes AGENTS.md and ci/checks/classify\_ci\_changes.py define; Kronos's planning defect, which QA-70 reviews v1.3 and v1.4 did not catch. Correction: PO-QA100-6, throwaway copy only; republished at 7974e2c7.  
  * O-1: the OPS03 tool's fixed RUN\_ROOT /tmp/hde-epic038-ops03 (tmpfs) and pytest's tmp\_path under TMPDIR (ext4) are on different filesystems, so a link or rename failed with EXDEV; with TMPDIR unset, as on hosted CI, both are under /tmp.  
  * QA planning and review cycles. QA-70 v1.0 of Plan v1.0 DENY (three blockers FND-001 to FND-003 and eight caveats; author defects); v1.1 of Plan v1.1 DENY (FND-101: common rule A made check 21's test copy unexecutable in every run; introduced by the revision and partly the reviewer's, since review v1.0's RL-05 omitted the coupled constraint); v1.2 of Plan v1.2 APPROVE; v1.3 of Plan v1.2 after the remedy DENY (FND-201 to FND-203: no commit had both the Plan's tested code and the remedy; FND-202 and FND-203 are Review Drift, the reviewer's, visible at ESC-40 when C-1 named the change); v1.4 of Plan v1.3 APPROVE. Route gap: addendum 2.27 C-1 required a Kronos-authored approved-Plan delta with a QA-70 delta-mode decision and no QA-80, but no prompt in release 100926.1 authors such a delta; the Product Owner decided the route instead (direction v1.1, D-1 to D-3; CR-12 APPROVED); recorded as MB-021 on the GCFPE Modification Backlog (GCFPE-MGMT-10 maintenance owner). Unresolved risk (CR-12): no PF10 addendum records the Plan v1.3 change; the QA-70 reviews v1.3 and v1.4 and the redline application report v1.2 do.  
  * Process deviations. PO-4: evidence of checks 1 to 15 was first left uncommitted in a scratch clone (QA-100 Execute step 4 commits evidence only under an explicit grant; collection v1.0 §4.8 left the grant to the Product Owner); persisted through [\#727](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/727) and [\#726](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/726); the QA-100 prompt defect is reported to the GCFPE-MGMT-10 maintenance owner. G-1 (the QA-100 gap, not Live QA Guide §8's G-1): a hand-typed captured\_at\_utc; checks 17 to 20 carry their real finalization times and collection v1.3 had check 21's read from date \-u.  
  * I-1 (session incident, not an evidence defect). About 2026-10-25T23:20Z, during check 16's local secret scan, a malformed export \$(\<command\>) line reduced to a bare export and printed the workstation environment, including HD\_API\_KEY, GEO\_API\_KEY, HD\_API\_BASE\_URL and DATABASE\_URL, into the executing session's transcript. It contradicts Glow QA Guide §3.5.7. No repository file, commit or published primary holds a value (every primary scanned NO\_HIT). The Product Owner decided not to rotate (PO-QA100-4). The executing session installed a PreToolUse hook on the workstation, \~/.claude/hooks/block-env-dump.py, denying bare export, env, printenv, set and declare \-x, export \$(\<command\>), reads of /proc/\*/environ and echo \$\<secret\>; proven to fire. The workstation's values are not shown to be production's, and the production service's values were never printed. Open: the transcript's handling (Product Owner); the hook is local to the workstation and is not a repository control.  
  * Other anomalies (recorded, not causes of any outcome). O-2, O-5 and O-12: every HDE-CRD-0002 path proof carries produced\_at\_utc 2026-09-22T14:25:29Z and republication keeps an earlier mtime\_utc; the validators accept both and AGENTS.md "Path-proof portability" treats mtime\_utc as provenance. O-9: the deployed identity's build\_commit 9479d28 is an older commit, recorded and not judged (identity is judged by release\_id). O-10: the "final LF no" label in the bodies of checks 16 to 19 is a display flag. The executors' TypeSafe readings could not be taken (HTTP 403).  
  * Patterns. (1) Venue assumptions not tested against the real operating environment: QA110-F01 (shallow validation venues), check 3 attempt 1, O-1. (2) Coupled constraints missed while writing or reviewing guards: FND-101, FND-202, FND-203, O-11.  
  * Remediation-loop assessment. QA110-F01 took the canonical route of Glow QA Guide §10.6 (QA-110 ESCALATION\_REQUIRED, ESC-10, ESC-30, ESC-40, WU-REM-02 through PR-30, PR-35 and PR-40, the Plan route of CR-12, and QA-90/QA-100/QA-110 for RV-1) with one deviation (C-1's delta route had no author prompt). No accepted check was reopened (checks 1 to 12, 14 and 15 stood at state (a); the remedy changed no release member). Each Moon Loop was bounded to the QA venue or a throwaway copy, and each superseded attempt is preserved byte for byte. Evidence hygiene: every primary was published through the canonical writer and validated by the five validators and validate\_crd\_check\_family; the Index and Mirror hold every key; no primary holds a secret value; the one assembly inaccuracy (G-1) is recorded rather than rewritten.  
  * Causal limits (stated by the RCA). The post-run inspection followed step 3 and could not show which .git file changed in attempt 1 or which step wrote it; the mechanism is established by reproduction (QA-110 v1.0's scratch-repository verification under git 2.43.0, ESC-30, ESC-40 and the remedy's V-3 negative control naming the pack's mtime and ctime), and RV-1 passing in the same venue class supports it; that attempt 1 was this mechanism and nothing else is the strongest supported reading, not a direct observation. Hosted CI's checkout depth is not established here. I-1's exposure beyond the transcript is not evidenced and not ruled out beyond what the scans cover.  
* Lessons. L-1 a tool that inspects .git must be validated on a full-history source as well as a shallow one (Implementation Agent and PR reviewers); L-2 a QA task's interpreter selection names a fallback the executor's venue can supply, and the collection records the venue it was authored against (Kronos, O-4); L-3 guards that exclude paths take the classifier's prefix list, not a subset (Kronos and QA-70 reviewers; D-1); L-4 when a remedy changes an approved Plan's tested source, list every guard and coupled constraint it touches in one pass (QA-70 reviewers; Kronos); L-5 timestamps in task specs are read from date \-u at assembly, never typed (Kronos); L-6 shell lines touching credentials are built without substitutions that can collapse to bare export, env or set, backed by a deny hook (executing sessions; Product Owner for workstation controls); L-7 the evidence-commit grant is settled before execution (Kronos in collections; GCFPE-MGMT-10 maintenance owner for QA-100).  
* TypeSafe reading for CL-C-10: v8.2 sent 2026-10-26T00:27:58Z reads Opus 5.5 at extra high (effort score 3.06, confidence 0.59; rungs low 0.01, medium 0.05, high 0.15, extra high 0.46, max 0.33; models Fable 5.1 0.03, Opus 5.5 0.75, Sonnet 5.5 0.22; model confidence 0.67; highest cells Opus 5.5 at extra high 0.345 and Opus 5.5 at max 0.247; flag: boundary between max and extra high; jev-1.13.0). The Notion row was not written; Nathan picks the cell; no pick is stated for CL-C-10 or QA-120 (its reading is in QA-110 review v1.2 §9, Opus 5.5 at extra high, score 3.30). Surface advice: CL-C-10 in the continuing Isis session on amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2 with Notion read access for the CL-C-10 prompt and the CL-20 receiver it names; reads the Report and the RCA first, then the evidence set (the Report's §13); addenda 2.19 to 2.28 in PF10 v13.8 record most of the lineage in one place; no production, Railway, vendor or database access and no execution; writes only the closure decision and its handoff under docs/ephemeral/ by pull request.

### Epic, task, and status effects

* The QA run is complete with verdict: PASS. Next prompt: CL-C-10 — Perform CRD Retrospective and Decide Closure — 100926.1, by the continuing Isis, which decides CLOSE or DO\_NOT\_CLOSE. That is a closure-oriented review result, not the closure decision and not a SATISFIED close-pack decision.

## 2.31 The change flow gains a close-mutation-set stage, CL-05, between QA-120 and the closure decision; it is Dev work and repairs its own close tooling

Timestamp: 102626 02:50 (UTC). Details: Records the Product Owner's finding of 2026-10-26: the close-pack machinery is built, but nothing in the prompt ecosystem, PF canon or the role set produces a change's close mutation set. It records his direction that the missing process be reusable and become part of the change flow, as a stage that repairs its own close tooling. The stage's prompt is written in Notion as a candidate for the next GCFPE Modification. Origin: the continuing HDE-CRD-0002 Isis session ([Isis-53](https://claude.ai/code/session_01NxRhYei7RDGmMPYdKEkpok)), after CL-C-10 v1.0 returned DO\_NOT\_CLOSE for the missing close mutation set.

### Approval and source record

* The Product Owner's words:  
  * "the gap seems to be that the close pack machinery is now built, but there is no actual canonical process for creating in either in the prompt ecosystem, canon, or anyone else. Record this as a pf10 build notes addendum, GCFPE feedback, and then create a task to create the close pack. The prompt should be created by you (Isis). I will need you to determine if this is an Ops or Dev task first. Either way, it will need to return to CL-C-10."  
  * On a draft that routed the close through ESC-30: "No, I don't want to do it this way. This will just loop back into the change flow. This needs to be a new process."  
  * "'reusable' but It needs to become PART of the change flow, that is what I wanted the PF10 addendum and GCFPE feedback to reflect."  
  * On who fixes a close-tooling defect: "The process fixes it".  
  * "You can write it in Notion as a new prompt, it can be used as a candidate when I run the next modification to the GCFPE".  
  * "the new prompt can just run in a standalone session".  
* Recorded by Isis at the Product Owner's direction. No approval prompt produced this addendum.  
* Where this addendum conflicts with an earlier addendum, an in-flight change document or a prompt's routing, it governs for the scope below.

### Retained decisions and requirements

1. The gap. These exist:  
   * the generic close-pack writer tools/qa/generate\_epic\_close\_pack.py and its v2 schema (addenda 2.15 and 2.17);  
   * the CRD close-run family, published by publish\_crd\_close\_run\_family;  
   * the manual closeout validation workflow (addendum 2.13).  
2. But no prompt, PF section or role produces a change's close mutation set with them. PF06 §3.5.1 and §3.5.2 require the close mutation set and the close PR, but name no producing stage, entry or owner. In release 100926.1:  
   * CL-C-10 and CL-E-10 route DO\_NOT\_CLOSE only to an existing owner or to ESC-30;  
   * QA-120 defers the close-pack measure;  
   * no prompt authors a close-candidate source.  
3. HDE-EPIC038 and HDE-EPIC040 closed by exceptional closure, and HDE-CRD-0002's closure decision had no next prompt.  
4. A new stage, part of the change flow. CL-05 — Prepare and Publish the Close Mutation Set serves every Epic and CRD.  
   * Entries:  
     * QA-120's final QA Report with verdict: PASS;  
     * a closure decision's DO\_NOT\_CLOSE whose return work is the close mutation set (missing, incomplete or defective).  
   * Exit: the closure decision (CL-C-10 or CL-E-10), after the Product Owner merges the close pull request.  
   * What it owns:  
     * the close mutation set: the close pack, its two ledgers, the same-run close evidence, and their Index and Mirror registration;  
     * any repair of the close tooling that the close needs.  
   * What it never does. It never reopens implementation, QA or the closure decision. Close-tooling repair uses no ESC, RS or PR-10 to PR-40 stage.  
5. Dev, not Ops. CL-05 is a PR work unit. It writes tracked repository state through the canonical writers, and its work lands by the Product Owner's merge:  
   * PF06 §3.5.2.1: "The close PR MUST include the epic close-pack (canonical filenames)";  
   * PF06 §3.5.2.3: the Index and Mirror trio "in the same authorized mutation set".  
6. An Ops task "requires privileged access to systems outside the repository", and "Ops tasks are not repository PRs" (PF27 §3; PF06 §0.2; PF04 §9.1). An OPS-01 run only packages "already-produced" close-pack artifacts (PF06 §0.2).  
7. Authority and actor.  
   * The Product Owner's invocation of CL-05 for a change authorizes that change's close mutation set and any close-tooling repair it needs, and is the Proceed for both.  
   * It runs as a standalone top-level session that the Product Owner starts with the stage page and the change ID. It needs no handoff to start and resolves its inputs from the change's records.  
   * The closure authority (Isis) judges its result at the closure decision. The Product Owner alone merges.  
8. Close-tooling repair.  
   * What counts as a defect. CL-05 first rehearses the close in a throwaway clone. A close-tooling defect is one where the tools refuse or fail on correct inputs, or lack an admission the change needs.  
   * How it is repaired. CL-05 repairs it in its own pull request:  
     * the smallest change to the close-tooling surface: the writer, its source schemas, the close-run and close-pack admission in the canonical updater, the closeout workflow and their tests;  
     * owner tests that reproduce the defect and prove the fix;  
     * an amendment to the close-pack ADR when the fix changes a decision the ADR records.  
   * Narrowing a recorded rule. A repair may narrow a close-tooling rule that the ADR or an earlier HDE Build Notes addendum records, but only where that rule refuses the change's own governed evidence of record. It keeps the rule's purpose and records the narrowing and its basis. Example: 2.15's "the feedback-dependency refusal kept", which refuses HDE-CRD-0002's QA check 3 primary path.  
   * Limits. A repair never changes approved QA evidence, product code or PF canon.  
9. Interim operation. Until a GCFPE Modification installs CL-05:  
   * the candidate page runs at the Product Owner's direct invocation: [https\://app.notion.com/p/4054590a05eb8155b301e2cb6dec31d5](https://app.notion.com/p/4054590a05eb8155b301e2cb6dec31d5);  
   * QA-120 PASS, and a closure decision's DO\_NOT\_CLOSE for the close mutation set, are read as routing to it;  
   * a change's Implementation Plan needs no change-local Close Gate work unit. Where a Plan has one, as HDE-CRD-0002's WU-PR-07 T0 to T7, CL-05 performs it.  
10. HDE-CRD-0002.  
    * CL-05's first run performs CL-C-10 v1.0's return work and repairs close-tooling defect B-4.  
    * Its result returns to CL-C-10. That decision records B-4, which supersedes CL-C-10 v1.0's "Nothing failed".

### Epic, task, and status effects

* No status change. HDE-CRD-0002 stays open under CL-C-10 v1.0 (DO\_NOT\_CLOSE).  
* No PF09, PF30 or board row changes.  
* Implementation Plan v1.1 is not edited. CL-05 performs its §7 WU-PR-07 T0 to T7.

### Required canon drainage

Owner of each: the Product Owner, through canon maintenance.

* Change Process Guide §3.5.1 and §3.5.2: name the close-mutation-set stage as part of the change flow, with its entries, its exit to the closure decision and its ownership of close-tooling repair. State that it is Dev, and that an Ops run only packages or proves already-produced artifacts.  
* Glow QA Guide §3.1.3: state that the close mutation set precedes the closure decision, outside the post-closure drainage rule.  
* Plan Templates: a close-mutation-set task template beside the Ops Task Record.

### Deferred obligations and unresolved work

* GCFPE installation. Install CL-05 as a release member with the routing its installation notes give:  
  * QA-120 pass\_crd and pass\_epic go to CL-05;  
  * CL-C-10 and CL-E-10 gain a DO\_NOT\_CLOSE branch back to CL-05 for the close mutation set, and read it as an input;  
  * a close-tooling defect is CL-05's to repair, not ESC-30's;  
  * a graph part, registry entry and catalog row.  
* Tracked as MB-030, with MB-027. Owner: the GCFPE-MGMT-10 maintenance owner, at the Product Owner's next Modification.  
* B-4 repair: CL-05's first run, for HDE-CRD-0002.  
* WU-PR-07 measure. Its "first attempt" clause cannot be met by the writer as accepted at [\#668](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/issues/668); the measure is observed on the repaired writer. Owner: the Product Owner.  
* PF10 numbering and publication: the Product Owner.

### Scope boundaries and nonclaims

* This addendum changes no selected prompt, skill, AGENTS.md, code, test, schema, graph part or permanent PF document, and edits no in-flight record.  
* The CL-05 page is a candidate. It is not a selected release member until the register selects it.  
* This addendum is not a QA verdict, acceptance, closure decision or Plan approval. It authorizes no close mutation set; the Product Owner's invocation of CL-05 does.

### Evidence anchors

* Direction: the Product Owner's words above, 2026-10-26, session [Isis-53](https://claude.ai/code/session_01NxRhYei7RDGmMPYdKEkpok).  
* Stage and feedback:  
  * the CL-05 candidate page, [https\://app.notion.com/p/4054590a05eb8155b301e2cb6dec31d5](https://app.notion.com/p/4054590a05eb8155b301e2cb6dec31d5);  
  * the GCFPE Modification Backlog's MB-030 and MB-027.  
* Records:  
  * docs/ephemeral/HDE-CRD-0002-CL-C-10-closure-decision-v1.0.md (pull request [\#747](https://github.com/amthorn78/glow-hdengine-v2/pull/747));  
  * docs/ephemeral/HDE-EPIC040-CL-E-10-closure-decision-v1.1.md;  
  * docs/ephemeral/HDE-EPIC040-CLOSE01-closure-evidence-task-prompt-v1.0.md;  
  * docs/ephemeral/GCFPE-alpha-feedback-epic-closure-evidence-task-v1.0.md.  
* Repository state on main 58a27859: no audit/HDE-CRD-0002\_\*, no audit/docdeltas/hde-crd-0002\_\* and no audit/qa/hde-crd-0002/close/.

### Relationship to existing HDE Build Notes guidance

* Addenda 2.13, 2.15 and 2.17. This addendum adds to them who produces a change's close mutation set and how the result returns. It keeps their rules, except that a CL-05 repair may narrow one as rule 5 limits.

  * Lessons: CL-L1 to CL-L7 stand. CL-L8: tooling that produces or validates close evidence should have its first real run, on the change's own evidence, inside the work unit that builds it; here the first real run came at close, after acceptance and QA, so its defect (B-4) surfaced at the Close Gate (owner: the GCFPE-MGMT-10 maintenance owner, MB-030, PR and IA prompts; the Product Owner). CL-L9: every process step that needs a hosted run names the repository event that starts it; "someone dispatches it" is not an event (B-5, B-6) (owner: the GCFPE-MGMT-10 maintenance owner, MB-030, CL-05).

## 2.34 Documentation-only changes are recorded in HDE Build Notes, not as CRD candidates; HDE-CRD-0002's stale repository documentation is corrected to the delivered state

Timestamp: 102626 18:55 (UTC). Details: Records the Product Owner's rule that a documentation change needing no development is decided in an HDE Build Notes addendum rather than carried as a CRD candidate. It applies the rule to the stale repository documentation that HDE-CRD-0002's records left with no owner (QA-10 triage FND-017 to FND-019; CL-05 O-1, O-2 and O-4; OBS-12), one further stale line found while applying it (D-3), and the S21 ADR's two open lines. For each it states the stale text, the current fact with its evidence on main 0adce9d9, and the corrected statement. Origin: the Product Owner's direction of 2026-10-26 in the continuing HDE-CRD-0002 Isis session, given in answer to the CL-40 scan's question on candidate item 11\.

### Approval and source record

* The Product Owner's direction, verbatim: "if something is a documentation change and not something that needs development, then you can create a PF10 build notes addendum to litigate it".  
* It answers the CL-40 cycle gap scan v1.0 §6, which asked whether candidate item 11 ("Repository documentation left stale after HDE-CRD-0002") should stay a CRD candidate or be routed as a documentation change. The scan recorded the item on the Candidate CRD Items List under the Product Owner's 2026-10-12 precedent, while inclusion criterion 1 of that list names no documentation target.  
* Change context: CRD HDE-CRD-0002, closed (CL-C-10 v1.2, CLOSE / CHANGE\_CLOSED; addendum 2.32). Nothing here reopens it.  
* Recorded by Isis (Isis-53), [https\://claude.ai/code/session\\\_01NxRhYei7RDGmMPYdKEkpok](https://claude.ai/code/session%5C_01NxRhYei7RDGmMPYdKEkpok). No approval prompt produced this addendum.

### Rule

1. Documentation-only changes. A change is documentation-only when it alters only repository documentation and needs no code, test, tool, workflow, schema, configuration or governed-evidence change. Repository documentation means prose files that describe the system, such as README.md, AGENTS.md, ARCHITECTURE.md, docs/\*\* and architecture decision records.  
   * Such a change is not a CRD candidate.  
   * An HDE Build Notes addendum records and decides it, stating for each change the stale text and its location, the current fact with its evidence, and the corrected statement.  
2. Development keeps its route. A finding that needs development remains a CRD candidate or PF09 work, as canon assigns it, even when it also touches documentation lines.  
3. Out of scope. PF canon documents keep their existing route: HDE Build Notes addenda, then drainage into the permanent PF home. Governed evidence and its writers are unaffected.  
4. Classification of the correction. Applying a correction is documentation/status drainage only (Change Process Guide §0.2). Its owner is the Product Owner. An AGENTS.md edit needs the Product Owner's explicit instruction.
